## ר (id: BDB04194)

\[Str4183] † רַשְׁתִּי(וֺ)מ **adj. gent.** only מִיכָה הַמֹּרַשְׁתִּי [Mi 1:1](https://ref.ly/Mic1:1), ´מִיכָי֯ה הַמּוֺ [Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18); v. foregoing.

> \[Str8492] † תִּירוֺשׁ **n.m.**:Ju [9:13](https://ref.ly/Judg9:13) must, fresh or new wine;—abs. תִּירוֺשׁ [Nu 18:12](https://ref.ly/Num18:12) \+ 24 t.; תִּירשׁ [Gn 27:28](https://ref.ly/Gen27:28) \+ 3 t.; sf. תִּירוֺשִׁי Ju [9:13](https://ref.ly/Judg9:13), [Ho 2:11](https://ref.ly/Hos2:11); תִּירשְׁ [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13) \+ 4 t. Dt; תִּירוֺשֵׁךְ [Is 62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8); תִּירוֺשָׁם [Psalm 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8); —*must, new wine,* as enlivening הַמְּשַמֵּחַ אֱלֽהִים וַאֲנָשִׁים ´ת Ju [9:13](https://ref.ly/Judg9:13); as injurious יִקַּח־לֵב ´וְנוּת וְיַיִן וְת [Ho 4:11](https://ref.ly/Hos4:11); poet. regarded as contained in the grapes יִמָּעֵא בָּאֶשְׁכּוֺל ´הַתּ [Is 65:8](https://ref.ly/Isa65:8), cf. [24:7](https://ref.ly/Isa24:7) (‖גֶּפֶן); as yielding wine (יַיִן), וְלאֹ תִּשְׁתֶּה ָ\+ייִן ´תִּדְרֹךְ זַיִת וְלאֹ תָסוּךְ שֶׁמֶן וְת [Mi 6:15](https://ref.ly/Mic6:15); usu. as a sign of fertility, or as valuable product: יְקָבֶיְ יִפְדָֹצוּ ´וְת [Pr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Prov3:10) *with must thy vats shall burst open;* \+ דָּגָן (q. v.) [Gn 27:28](https://ref.ly/Gen27:28), [27:37](https://ref.ly/Gen27:37) (both J) [Ho 2:11](https://ref.ly/Hos2:11), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14), [Zc 9:17](https://ref.ly/Zech9:17), [Psalm 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8), [Is 62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8); ´ת [Ho 9:2](https://ref.ly/Hos9:2) (‖גֹּרֶן, יֶקֶב cf. דָּגָן v:[1](https://ref.ly/Hos9:1)); ´וְת ´אֶרֶץ ד [Dt 33:28](https://ref.ly/Deut33:28); \+ יִצְהָר [Jo 2:24](https://ref.ly/Joel2:24), [Ne 10:38](https://ref.ly/Neh10:38) (\+ פְּרִי כָל־עֵץ); \+ דָּגָן and יִצְהָר [Ho 2:10](https://ref.ly/Hos2:10), [3:24](https://ref.ly/Hos3:24), [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Deut11:14), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Deut12:17), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), [28:51](https://ref.ly/Deut28:51), [Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12), [Hg 1:11](https://ref.ly/Hag1:11), [Jo 1:10](https://ref.ly/Joel1:10), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Joel2:19), [2 Ch 32:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:28), [Ne 5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11), [10:40](https://ref.ly/Neh10:40), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Neh13:5), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Neh13:12); ´חֵלֶב ת [Nu 18:12](https://ref.ly/Num18:12) (v. ´ח; \+ *id.*); ´ת [2 Ch 31:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:5) \+ דגן, יצהר, and דְּבַשׁ; אֶרֶץ לֶחֶם וּכְרָמִים ´אֶרֶץ דָּגָן וְת [Is 36:17](https://ref.ly/Isa36:17) \= [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) ( \+ ארץ זית יצהר ודבשׁ).
> 
> 440:b. תִּירוֺשׁ: on mng., v. ARSKEncy. Bib. WINE (iv. 5307 f.).

## רָאַם (id: BDB08823)

\[Str7213,7311,7414] †\[רָאַם] **vb.** (Mass Thes al.) rise (si vera l., ‖ form of רום);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. רָאֲֽמָה [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10), but \< rd. רָאמָה \= רָ֫מָה (√רום), Ges§ 72 p.

> \[Str7214] †רְאֵם **n.m.**: [Jb 39:10](https://ref.ly/Job39:10) wild ox (As. *rêmu* DlHWB 603 (ראם), HoughtonTSBA v (1877\). 336 ff., and illustr. bef. p. 33 SchrKGF 135 ff., 530 Hom: NS 237 ff., 410, 436 f. Dr:[Dt 33:17](https://ref.ly/Deut33:17); on strength and ferocity, PlinNH viii. 21; Aram. רְאֵמָא, רֵימָא, רֵימָנָא ܪܰܝܡܳܐ (LagBN 58\); Ar. رَأْمَةٌ is *white antelope,* antilope leucoryx; LXX (erron.) μονοκέρως ([Is 34:7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7) ἅδροι, Vulgate *unicornis,* and (oftener) *rhinoceros*);—abs. ´ר: [Nu 23:22](https://ref.ly/Num23:22) \+, רְאֵים [Psalm 92:11](https://ref.ly/Ps92:11), רֵים [Jb 39:9](https://ref.ly/Job39:9), [39:10](https://ref.ly/Job39:10); pl. רְאֵמִים [Is 34:7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7), [Psalm 29:6](https://ref.ly/Ps29:6), רֵמִים [22:22](https://ref.ly/Ps22:22) (v. Baer);—*wild ox,* as fierce and strong [Jb 39:9](https://ref.ly/Job39:9), [39:10](https://ref.ly/Job39:10); sim. of strength of Isr., לוֺ ´כְּתוֺעֲפוֺת ר: [Nu 23:22](https://ref.ly/Num23:22) \= [24:8](https://ref.ly/Num24:8) (JE), וַתָּרֶם כִּרְאֵם קַרְנִי [Psalm 92:11](https://ref.ly/Ps92:11); so fig. of Joseph, קַרְנַיו ´קַרְנֵי ר: [Dt 33:17](https://ref.ly/Deut33:17); fig. of princes of Edom Is 347 ( \+פָּרִים עִם אַבִּירִים); of powerful foes, מִקַּרְנֵי רֵמִים [Psalm 22:22](https://ref.ly/Ps22:22); in sim. of skipping, leaping, בֶּן־רְאֵמִים [Psalm 29:6](https://ref.ly/Ps29:6) (‖עֵגֶּל).

> \[Str7208] †רְאוּמָה **n.pr.f.** concubine of Nahor [Gn 22:23](https://ref.ly/Gen22:23) (J); A Ρεηρα, LXXL Ρεημα.

> \[Str7215] †I. רָאמוֺת **n.\[f.pl.]** usu. corals (so Ki; *black corals* Thes (פְּנִינִים \= *red corals*); cf. Ar. رَأْسٌ, *sea\-shell,* WeHeid. 2\. 163\);—as costly [Jb 28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18), as merchandise, רָאמֹת Ez [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16);—רָאמוֺת לֶאֱוִיל חָכְמוֺת [Pr 24:7](https://ref.ly/Prov24:7), reading and sense dub., v. Toy.

> \[Str7213,7216,7414,7418] רָֽאֲמָה, II. רָאמוֺת, רָאמַת v. רום, II. רמה. above, p. 728, 926, 928

> \[Str7326,7389] רָאשׁ *poor,* רֵאשׁ *poverty,* v. רושׁ. P.930

## I ראֹשׁ (id: BDB08829)

\[Str7218,7226,4763,7219–20] I. ראֹשׁ:599 **n.m.**:[Lv 13:45](https://ref.ly/Lev13:45) head (common Sem. word; earliest form *\*ra’š*, Ar. ርእስ፡, Sab. ראס PräZMG xxix (1875\). 425 MordtmHim.Inschr. 31; Eth. ርኣስ፡ Amh. hence (*a* heightened) As. *rêšu* (rarely *râšu*), Aram. רֵישָׁא (Egypt. Aram. ראש CookeNorth\-Sem. Inscr. 404\), ܪܺܝܫܳܐ, mod. *(Ma‘lûla) raïša,* HuartJAs xii (1878\), 491 Duvalid. xiii(1879\), 464; Palm. רשא, Lzb366, cf. BH רִישׁוֺן, רֵאשִׁית; fr.*\*ra’š*, also BH \*רָאשׁ, ראֹשׁ (LagSymm. i. 113\), Tel Am. *rušu(nu)* Wkl189, 18 (gloss to *ḳaḳḳadunu*), NH \= BH; SI6 ראש, Ph. רשׁ, ראש (in n.pr.), Pun. *rus* (in n.pr. loc.) SchrödPh. Spr.133, ראשת *choicest, chief;* on MI20 (sf.) רשה, l.:28 ש\[ר], cf. Sm. u. So14 NöLCB Jan. 8, 1887, 60\);—´ר: abs. [Gn 3:15](https://ref.ly/Gen3:15) \+, cstr. ## I רָבַב (id: BDB08842)

\[Str7231] † I. \[רָבַב] **vb.** be or become, many, much (NH רַב esp. *lord, master*, רַבִּי *my master teacher;* MI5 adj. pl. רבן; Ph רב f. רבת; As. \[*rabâbu*], *rabbu;* Ar. رَبَّ *rear, increase* (act.; رُبٌ *thick juice*, cf. NH רְבָב *grease* is thought by Buhl al. to indicate original mng. *be thick*), رَبٌّ *lord, owner, master;* Sab. רב esp. in n. pr., רבאל, רבבם רבשמסם, etc., CISiv,nos\-285\. 3; 286,1; 287,12, etc.; Lihy. רבה *its lord* DHMEpigr.Denkm.Ar.232; Eth ረበበ፡ *expund, spread* (intrans.); Aram. רַב ܪܒ *great, chief*, so OAram. Nab. Palm. רב, Lzb366 Cook107);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. consec. וְרַבָּה [Ex 23:29](https://ref.ly/Exod23:29), [Is 6:12](https://ref.ly/Isa6:12), רָבָּה [Gn 18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) ([Ho 9:7](https://ref.ly/Hos9:7) rd. וְרֹב הַמּשְטֵמָה \[for MT ´וְרַבָּה מ:] LXX. We Now); elsewh. only 3 pl. רַבּוּ [1 S 25:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:10)\+, רָבּוּ [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23)\+2 t. \[other forms supplied by רבה q.v.]; *Inf.cstr* רֹב [Gn 6:1](https://ref.ly/Gen6:1), [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13), [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16) (‖מְעֹט), sf. רֻבְּכֶם [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7), and perh. רֻבָּם [Ho 4:7](https://ref.ly/Hos4:7) (cf. רֹב infr.);—**1\.** *be (become) many,* Pers. [Gn 6:1](https://ref.ly/Gen6:1) (J) [Ex 23:29](https://ref.ly/Exod23:29) (E; v.supr.), [1 S 25:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:10), [Ho 4:7](https://ref.ly/Hos4:7) (perh.), [Is 66:16](https://ref.ly/Isa66:16), [Psalm 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ps3:2), [25:19](https://ref.ly/Ps25:19), [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ps38:20), [Ec 5:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:10)\+ מִן comp. [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23), [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7), [Psalm 69:5](https://ref.ly/Ps69:5); of things [Is 22:9](https://ref.ly/Isa22:9), [Psalm 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8), 104:24, years [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16)(P), sins [Je 5:6](https://ref.ly/Jer5:6), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Jer14:7), [Is 59:12](https://ref.ly/Isa59:12), [Jb 35:6](https://ref.ly/Job35:6) . **2\.** *become great*, [Gn 18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) (J) [Is 6:12](https://ref.ly/Isa6:12); *be long,* of journey, [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13)—**Pu.** v. \[רָבַב] **denom** p.914, 1127

## רָבַךְ (id: BDB08867)

\[Str7246] † \[רָבַךְ] **vb.** mix, stir (Ar. رَبَكَ *mix, mingle;* NH רְבִיכָה, Targum רָבִיכָא \[for BH מֻרְבֶּכֶת], (dough) *mixed,* or *stirred*);—**Hoph.** *Pt.* f. as term. techn. ## רָבַע (id: BDB08879)

\[Str7250,7252] † \[רָבַע] **vb.** lie stretched out, lie down (Aram. form of רָבַץ (q.v.); NH רבע usu. of copulation, chiefly unnatural; yet cf. also Sab. רבע *abide, encamp, settle*, DHMZMG xxix (1875\), 593, Ar. رَبَع *abide, dwell*)—**Qal** only *Inf.:* sf. רִבְעִי [Psalm 139:3](https://ref.ly/Ps139:3) *my lying down* (for repose, opp.אָרְחִי); elsewh. (H) for copulation (woman with beast) עְרִבְעָה אֹתָהּ [Lv 20:16](https://ref.ly/Lev20:16), but read אִתָּהּ (sf. of beast) Dr\-Wh KöSynt. 226 e, cf. Bae Berthol (and Dr:[2 S 13:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:14)); לְרִבְעָהּ [18:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:23), rd. לְרִבְעָה (compl. om.; v. *id.*). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. בְּהֶמְתְּךָ לאֹ תַרְבִּיעַ כִּלְאַיִם [Lv 19:19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19) (H) *thy cattle thou shalt not cause to* (let) *lie down* (i.e. breed) in *two kinds.*

> \[Str7254] † II. רֶ֫בַע **n.m.** a king of Midian [Nu 31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), [Jos 13:21](https://ref.ly/Josh13:21); Ποβοκ,Ποβε(κ).

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 13:21; Leviticus 19:19; 2 Samuel 13:14; Leviticus 20:16; Numbers 31:8; 2 Samuel 18:23; Psalms 139:3


## רָבַץ (id: BDB08881)

\[Str7257] † רָבַץ **vb.** stretch oneself out, lie down, lie stretched out (NH \= BH; As. *rabâṣu, lie,dwell; rubṣu* (in word\-lists), *stall,* also *womb* (cf. II. רבע supr.); Ar. رَبِضَ *lie down, on the breast, stretch oneself out*, مرابض Sab. מרבצַן CISiv. no. 5, 2; Targum רְבֵע *lie stretched out*, Syr. ܪܒܥ, ChrPal ܪܒܥ *recline at meals*, Schwallyidlot. 87\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ר: [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9), 3 fs. consec. וְרָֽבְצָה [Dt 29:19](https://ref.ly/Deut29:19), רָבָצָה Ez [19:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:2), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְבָּץ [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6), [27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10); 3 fs. וַחִרְבַּץ [Nu 22:27](https://ref.ly/Num22:27); 3 mpl. יִרְבּצוּ [11:7](https://ref.ly/Num11:7) יִרְבָּצוּ [14:30](https://ref.ly/Num14:30) יִרְבָּצָוּן [Zp 2:7](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:7), [Psalm 104:22](https://ref.ly/Ps104:22); 3 fpl. תִּרְבַּצְנָה Ez [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14); *Pt.* רֹבֵץ [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5) \+ 3 t.; f. רֹבֶ֫צֶת [Gn 49:25](https://ref.ly/Gen49:25) \+ 2 t.; mpl. רֹבְצֹים [29:2](https://ref.ly/Gen29:2); —*lie down, lie:* of domestic animals, ass [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5) (E; under heavy burden), [Nu 22:27](https://ref.ly/Num22:27) (Je; in obstructed path), [Gn 49:14](https://ref.ly/Gen49:14) (at ease; poet., in sim.); sheep, in repose, [29:2](https://ref.ly/Gen29:2) (J), [Is 17:2](https://ref.ly/Isa17:2), [Zp 2:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:14); fig. of people Ez [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14); calf. in repose [Is 27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10): of wild beasts, lion, in lair, [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9) (poem in J; fig.), [Psalm 104:22](https://ref.ly/Ps104:22); \= make lair, abode Ez [19:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:2) (fig.), so of צִיִּים [Is 13:21](https://ref.ly/Isa13:21), תַּנֵין Ez [29:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3) (fig.); leopard (with kid) [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6), cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa11:7); \= brood, of mother\-bird [Dt 22:6](https://ref.ly/Deut22:6) (עַל־הָאֶפְרֹחִים); of man, in repose [Is 14:30](https://ref.ly/Isa14:30), [Jb 11:19](https://ref.ly/Job11:19), cf. [Zp 2:7](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:7), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:13) (fig. of flock perhaps. impl., cf. Ez [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14) supr.); of the deep, רֹבֶצֶת תָּחַת [Gn 49:25](https://ref.ly/Gen49:25), [Dt 33:13](https://ref.ly/Deut33:13); fig. of curse [Dt 29:19](https://ref.ly/Deut29:19) (ב Pers.); of sin, לַפֶּתַח חַטָאת רֹבֵץ [Gn 4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7) *at the door sin makes its lair.* **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יַרְבִּיצֵנִי [Psalm 23:2](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2), 2 ms. תַּרְבִּיץ Ct [1:7](https://ref.ly/Song1:7), etc.; *Pt.* מַרְבִּיץ Is 54:1l, pl. מַרְבִּצִים [Je 33:12](https://ref.ly/Jer33:12);—*cause to lie down,* or *lie*, acc. of flock (for repose) [Je 33:12](https://ref.ly/Jer33:12), also (fig.) [Psalm 23:2](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2), Ez [34:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:15); acc. of flock om. [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10), Ct [1:7](https://ref.ly/Song1:7); of *laying* stones Is 54:1l.

> \[Str7258] † רֵ֫בֶץ **n.** \[**m.**] (place of) lying down, resting\- or dwelling\-place;—cstr. בָּקָר ´ר: [Is 65:10](https://ref.ly/Isa65:10) (‖נְוֵה צאֹן); sf. בִּנְוֵה תַנִּים רִבְצָהּ [35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7) (cf. Ges:§145m, but Du רָֽבְצָה, sc. flock); רִבְצָם [Je 50:6](https://ref.ly/Jer50:6) (people as sheep); of man, רִבְצוֺ [Pr 24:15](https://ref.ly/Prov24:15) (‖נְוֵה צַדְּיק).

> \[Str4769] † מַרְבֵּץ **n.** \[**m.**].;—of wild beasts, לַחַיָּה ´מ: [Zp 2:15](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:15) (sign of desolation, ‖שַׁמָּה); of flock, cstr. מִרְבַּץ־צאֹן Ez [25:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:5) (‖נְוֵה גְמַלִּים).

## רבק (id: BDB08884)

> \[Str7259] רִבְקָה:30 **n.pr.f.** Rebekah, daughter of Bethuel and wife of Isaac, [Gn 22:23](https://ref.ly/Gen22:23), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Gen24:15) \+ 24 t. 24, 25, 26, 27; [28:5](https://ref.ly/Gen28:5), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Gen29:12), [35:8](https://ref.ly/Gen35:8), [49:31](https://ref.ly/Gen49:31);—LXX Πεβεκκα,.

## רגב (id: BDB08888)

רגב (√ of following).

> \[Str7263] † \[רֶ֫גֶב] **n.m.** clod of earth;—pl. רְגָכִיְ יְדֻבָּקוּ [Jb 38:38](https://ref.ly/Job38:38) *clods are joined together;* cstr. רְגְבֵי נָחַָל [21:33](https://ref.ly/Job21:33) *clods of* (the) *wady*.

> \[Str709] † אַרְגֹּב **n.pr. 1\. loc.** (*heap*, or *region of clods; ‘glede’* GASmG551);—always ´חֶבֶל א: [Dt 3:4](https://ref.ly/Deut3:4), [3:14](https://ref.ly/Deut3:14), [1 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13), ´הָא :´ח: [Dt 3:13](https://ref.ly/Deut3:13); \= *measured region of Argōb;* some well\-defined district of Bashan (appar. identif. with חַוֹּת יָזִיר [Dt 3:14](https://ref.ly/Deut3:14) \[where ´י :´ח: in Bashan, so [Jos 13:30](https://ref.ly/Josh13:30)], but this a harmonistic correction; ´י :´ח: in fact in Gilead \[[Nu 32:41](https://ref.ly/Num32:41), Ju [10:4](https://ref.ly/Judg10:4), and disting. from אַרְגֹּב [1 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13) cf. [Dt 3:13](https://ref.ly/Deut3:13)); exact loc. dubious, GASmi.c. and esp. ## רָגַז (id: BDB08892)

\[Str7264] † רָגַז **vb.** be agitated, quiver, quake, be excited, perturbed (Ph. Iph. (\+ Inf. abs. Qal) *disquiet, disturb*, Inscr. Tabn.:4, 6, 7; NH Hiph. *provoke to wrath;* رجز, *tremble* (with rage, fear, Dozy); V, VIII, *rumble* (of thunder, Frey), رَجَزٌ *a trembling disease* (of camels), etc.; Aram. רְגַו *tremble, rage*, ܪܓܰܙ *be enraged;* Zinj. רגז *wrath*, Lzb 367\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. consec. וְרָגַז [Pr 29:9](https://ref.ly/Prov29:9), 3 fs. רָֽגְזָה [Is 14:9](https://ref.ly/Isa14:9) \+, etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְגַּו [2 S 7:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:10), [1 Ch 17:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:9), 2 fs.וַתִּרְגְּוִי Ez [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43) (but v. infr.); 3 mp!. יִרְגְּזוּן Hb [3:7](https://ref.ly/Hab3:7), יִרְגָּ\+זוּן [Ex 15:14](https://ref.ly/Exod15:14), etc.; *Imv.* ms. רְגָ֫וָה (Ges§ 48 i) Is 32:1l, mpl. רִהְזוּ [Psalm 4:5](https://ref.ly/Ps4:5);—*quake*, subj. אֶרֶץ [1 S 14:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:15), [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8) (עַל rei), [Psalm 77:19](https://ref.ly/Ps77:19) (\+ רָעַשׁ), [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/John2:10) (לִפְנֵי of locusts; ‖רלשׁ), [Pr 30:21](https://ref.ly/Prov30:21)(תַּחַת Pers.); subj. מוֺסְדֵי הָרִים [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) (‖רלשׁ), \= [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) (מוֺסְדוֺת הַשָׁמַיִם; ‖ *id.*); הֶהָרִים [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25); תְּהֹמוֺת [Psalm 77:17](https://ref.ly/Ps77:17); of tent\-curtains Hb [3:7](https://ref.ly/Hab3:7) (fig. of terror of tent\-dwellers); of people, in dread, c. מִפְּנֵי Pers. [Dt 2:25](https://ref.ly/Deut2:25) (‖תוּל), [Is 64:1](https://ref.ly/Isa64:1); c. עַל rei [Je 33:9](https://ref.ly/Jer33:9) (\+ פָּחַד); abs., [Ex 15:14](https://ref.ly/Exod15:14) (‖ ´חִיל אָחַז וגו:), [Psalm 99:1](https://ref.ly/Ps99:1), Jo 2:l; pregn. \= *come quivering* [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17) (מִן loc.); of Pers., in fear, awe, [Gn 45:24](https://ref.ly/Gen45:24) (ב loc.), [Is 32:11](https://ref.ly/Isa32:11) (‖הָרַד), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Isa32:10), [Psalm 4:5](https://ref.ly/Ps4:5), Hb [3:16](https://ref.ly/Hab3:16) (תַּחְתַּי), cf. of בִּטְבִי Hb [3:16](https://ref.ly/Hab3:16); of Israel, \= *be disquieted,* [2 S 7:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:10) \= [1 Ch 17:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:9); *be excited, perturbed,* of Pers. 2 S [19:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:1) (by grief; cf. Dr), שְׁאוֺל [Is 14:9](https://ref.ly/Isa14:9) (surprise ; ל Pers.); in rage, c. ל, *at*, Ez [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43) (of Jerus. personif.; but rd. **Hiph.** (i.e. *didst enrage me*) LXX SyrVer Vulgate Targum Hi Sm Co Berthol Toy Krae); prob. also וְשָחַק ´וְר: [Pr 29:9](https://ref.ly/Prov29:9); of ´י: Is 28:2l. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִרְגִּיז [Is 23:11](https://ref.ly/Isa23:11); 2 ms. sf. הִרְגַּוְחַנִי [1 S 28:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:15); *Impf.* 1 S.ארְגִּיו [Is 13:13](https://ref.ly/Isa13:13); *Inf. cstr.* הִרְגִּיו (Ges§ 53 l) [Je 50:34](https://ref.ly/Jer50:34); *Pt.* מִרְגִּיז [Is 14:16](https://ref.ly/Isa14:16), [Jb 9:6](https://ref.ly/Job9:6), pl. cstr. מַרְגִּיוֵי [12:6](https://ref.ly/Job12:6);—*cause to quake, disquiet, enrage: cause* earth *to quake* [Is 14:16](https://ref.ly/Isa14:16) (fig.; ‖רעשׁ), [Jb 9:6](https://ref.ly/Job9:6) *shake earth* מִמְּקוֺמָתּ; heavens [Is 13:13](https://ref.ly/Isa13:13) (‖רעשׁ); kingdoms [23:11](https://ref.ly/Isa23:11); *cause disquiet*, c. ל Pers., [Je 50:34](https://ref.ly/Jer50:34) (‖רגע); \= disturb [1 S 28:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:15) (cf. Ph. תרגזן, Inscr. Tabn.:4 DrSm xxviii);\= enrage, provoke, מַרְגִּיוֵי אֵל [Jb 12:6](https://ref.ly/Job12:6); so also prob. Ez [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43) (rdg. תַּרְגִּוִי לִי), v. **Qal** ad fin. **Hithp.** *excite oneself*, only *Inf.cstr.* sf. אלי הִתְרַגֶּוְךָ *thine exciting thyself* (to rage) against me, [2 K 19:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:27), [19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26) \= [Is 37:28](https://ref.ly/Isa37:28), [37:29](https://ref.ly/Isa37:29).

> \[Str7267] † רֹ֫גֶז **n.m.** [Jb 3:26](https://ref.ly/Job3:26) agitation, excitement, raging;—´ר: abs. Hb [3:2](https://ref.ly/Hab3:2) \+, cstr. [Jb 37:2](https://ref.ly/Job37:2); sf. רָנְזֶךָ [Is 14:3](https://ref.ly/Isa14:3);—*raging* [Jb 3:17](https://ref.ly/Job3:17); *disquiet, turmoil* [Is 14:3](https://ref.ly/Isa14:3), [Jb 3:26](https://ref.ly/Job3:26), [14:1](https://ref.ly/Job14:1); *raging, wrath* Hb [3:2](https://ref.ly/Hab3:2); קֹלוֺ ´ר: [Jb 37:2](https://ref.ly/Job37:2) *rumbling of his voice* (i.e. thunder); of *excitement* of warhorse, בְּרַעַשׁ וְרֹגֶּו [39:24](https://ref.ly/Job39:24).

> \[Str7269] † רָגְזָה **n.f.** a quivering, quaking;—´ר: Ez [12:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:18) (‖רַעַשׁ).

> \[Str7268] † רַגָּז **adj.** quivering, quaking;—´לֵב ר: [Dt 28:65](https://ref.ly/Deut28:65) *a quaking heart*.

> \[Str712] † אֶרְגַּז **n.m.** box, chest, or like receptacle (cf. Ar. رِجَازَةٌ *a kind of* (camel\-) *vehicle for women*, also *a garment containing stones*, etc., as balance, makeweight (at side of *haudaj*); from above √, as *swaying ?* Syr. ܪܳܓܽܘܙܬܳܐ *sack,* Talm. אַרְגָּו *chest, coffin*);—´הָא: [1 S 6:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:8), [6:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:11), [6:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:15), cf. HPS.

## רָגַע (id: BDB08910)

\[Str7280] † רָגַע **vb.** disturb (NH Hiph. *move to and fro;* perh. transp. from Ar. رَعَجَ *disturb,* Baes 8\);—**Qal** *Pf.* [Jb 26:12](https://ref.ly/Job26:12) בְּכֹחוֺ רָגַע הַיָם; *Pt. cstr.* (Ges65d. 116 g. x), [Je 31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35) \= [Is 51:15](https://ref.ly/Isa51:15) רֹגַע הַיָם וַיֶהֱמוּ גַלָּיו. **Hiph.** denom. from רֶבַע *make a twinkling,* only 1 S. Impf. cohort. וְעַד אִרְגִּ֫יעָה לְשׁוֺן שָׁקֶר and *while I would twinkle* (\= *only for a moment:* cf. [Jb 20:5](https://ref.ly/Job20:5)) is the false tongue opp. תִּכּוֺן לָעַד, [Pr 12:19](https://ref.ly/Prov12:19) (cf. De); and כִּי אִרְגִּ֫יעָה. אֲרִיעֶנּוּ מֵעָלֶיתָ *I will twinkle and* (\= I will *in a moment*, Ges§ 120 g; cf. Hi) chase them away from it, [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19) \= [50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44). (Ba:§ 86 as subst. \= רֶגַע, of the very rare type *’aqtîl;* ה\-; as Ges§ 90 f.)

> \[Str7281] † רֶ֫גַע **n.m.** a moment (NH *id.;*Targum ריגעא [Ec 9:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:12): prob. properly a *movement*, i.e. *twinkling*, of the eye; cf. *momentum*, i.e. *movimentum*);—abs. ´ר:, רָנַע [Nu 16:21](https://ref.ly/Num16:21) \+, pl. רְגָעִים;—**a.** [Psalm 30:6](https://ref.ly/Ps30:6) בְּאַפּוֺ ´ר: a moment (passes) in his anger (opp. חיים ברצונו); [Is 54:7](https://ref.ly/Isa54:7) קָטֹן ´בְּר: during *a little moment* **b.** usu. in adv. phrases:—(a) as adv. acc., (a) [Ex 33:5](https://ref.ly/Exod33:5) אֶהָד ´ר: *for one moment*, so ´ר: alone [Is 54:8](https://ref.ly/Isa54:8); repeated [Je 18:7](https://ref.ly/Jer18:7), [18:9](https://ref.ly/Jer18:9) רֶגַע…רֶגַע: *at another moment;* … *at another moment;* (β) *in a moment, suddenly,* [Psalm 6:11](https://ref.ly/Ps6:11) יָשׁוּבוּ יֵבשׁוּ רָגַע, [Je 4:20](https://ref.ly/Jer4:20), [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [Jb 34:20](https://ref.ly/Job34:20). (*b*) בְּרֶגַע *in a moment* [Jb 21:13](https://ref.ly/Job21:13) שְׁאוֺל יַחַ֫תּוּ ´ובר: (of a quick and painless death; but see [34:20](https://ref.ly/Job34:20), [Psalm 73:19](https://ref.ly/Ps73:19): Hoffm Buhl Beer Bu Du \[cf. LXX ἐν ἀναπαύσει, Targum בְּמַרְגּוֺעָא] take רֶגַע (or rd. רְגֹעַ) as subst. \[√ II. רָגַע] in tranquility, of a peaceful death). So (*c*) ´כְּמוֺ ר: [Nu 16:21](https://ref.ly/Num16:21) \= [17:10](https://ref.ly/Num17:10) וַאֲכַלֶּה אֶתֵכֶם כְּרָגַע, [Psalm 73:19](https://ref.ly/Ps73:19). (*d*) ´כְּמוֺ ר: [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6). (*e*) עֲדֵי רָגַע while *a moment* lasts [Jb 20:5](https://ref.ly/Job20:5). (*f*) ´כִּמְעַט ר: *like* the littleness of *a moment* \= *for* a little *moment* [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20), [Ezr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:8). (*g*) pl. לִרְגָעִים *by moments, at every moment*, [Is 27:8](https://ref.ly/Isa27:8), Ez [26:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:16) ´וְחָֽרְדוּ ל: (so [32:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:10)), [Jb 7:18](https://ref.ly/Job7:18) (‖לִבְקָרִים).

Ol:§ 236 e, and, further, Köi. 160\), sf. יִרְ דְָּפֶָךָ Ez [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6), etc.; *Imv.* ms. רְדֹף [Gn 44:4](https://ref.ly/Gen44:4) \+, sf. רָדְפֵחוּ [Psalm 34:15](https://ref.ly/Ps34:15); mpl. רִדְפוּ Ju [3:28](https://ref.ly/Judg3:28) \+; *Inf. cstr.* רָדְפִף [Jos 8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16) \+, מִרְדֹף [1 S 23:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:28), [2 S 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:16), sf. רָדְפִי [Psalm 38:21](https://ref.ly/Ps38:21) Qr, רדופי Kt, etc.; *Pt.* רֹדֵף Ju [4:22](https://ref.ly/Judg4:22) \+, pl. רֹדְפִים [8:4](https://ref.ly/Judg8:4) \+;—**1\. a.** *pursue* (sts. \+ הִשִּיג): lit. c. אַחֲרֵי Pers., in order to overtake [Gn 44:4](https://ref.ly/Gen44:4) (J), [2 K 5:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:21); esp. with hostile purpose [Gn 31:23](https://ref.ly/Gen31:23) (\+ הִדְבִּיק), [35:5](https://ref.ly/Gen35:5), [Jos 24:6](https://ref.ly/Josh24:6) (all E), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Josh2:5), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Josh2:7), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Josh2:7), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Josh8:17), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Josh10:19) (JE), [Dt 11:4](https://ref.ly/Deut11:4), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6), [Jos 20:5](https://ref.ly/Josh20:5) (all D), [Ex 14:4](https://ref.ly/Exod14:4), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Exod14:8), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Exod14:9) (P), Ju [1:6](https://ref.ly/Judg1:6), [2 S 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:6), [20:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:7), [20:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:10), [20:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:13), [2 K 9:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:27) \+ 17 t., \+ [2 K 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:5) (אַחַר), \+ Ju [4:16](https://ref.ly/Judg4:16) (אַחֲרֵי הָרֶכֶב); \+ רִדְפוּ אַחֲרֵי Ju [3:28](https://ref.ly/Judg3:28) \= *follow me* (as leader; but this meaning not elsewhere; rd. רְדוּ LXX Kit GFM). **b.** lit., c. acc. Pers., *put to flight, chase* (defeated foe; sts. c. הִשִּׁיג): [Am 1:11](https://ref.ly/Amos1:11), [Ho 8:3](https://ref.ly/Hos8:3), [Dt 32:30](https://ref.ly/Deut32:30) (poem; ‖הֵנִיס), [28:22](https://ref.ly/Deut28:22), [28:45](https://ref.ly/Deut28:45), [Jos 23:10](https://ref.ly/Josh23:10) (D), [Lv 26:8](https://ref.ly/Lev26:8), [26:8](https://ref.ly/Lev26:8) (H); וְרָדַף אֹתָם קוֺל עָלֶה נִדָּף v:[36](https://ref.ly/Lev26:36) a (H); [Jos 7:5](https://ref.ly/Josh7:5), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Josh8:24), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Josh10:10), [11:8](https://ref.ly/Josh11:8) (all JE), [Gn 14:15](https://ref.ly/Gen14:15), [Dt 1:44](https://ref.ly/Deut1:44), Ju [4:22](https://ref.ly/Judg4:22), [Is 41:3](https://ref.ly/Isa41:3) \+ 13 t. \+ Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25) (rd. אֶ־ת for אֶ־ל, LXX Vulgate Targum GFM Bu Now). † **c.** lit., acc. om. [La 1:3](https://ref.ly/Lam1:3); [Psalm 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6) (\+ הִשִּיג; v. supr.); esp. subj. ´י:, *pursue* (fig. of punishment, judgment), c. acc. Pers. [Je 29:18](https://ref.ly/Jer29:18) (בַּחֶרֶב), [La 3:43](https://ref.ly/Lam3:43) and (בְּאַף) v:[66](https://ref.ly/Lam3:66), [Psalm 83:16](https://ref.ly/Ps83:16) (בְּסַעֲרֶָךָ); אֶת־קַשׁ יָבֵשׁ תִּרְדֹּף [Jb 13:25](https://ref.ly/Job13:25) (תַּעַרוֺ ץ); רֹדְפָם, of ´מַלְאַךְ י:, [Psalm 35:6](https://ref.ly/Ps35:6). † **f.** fig. *persecute, harass,* c. acc. Pers. [Dt 30:7](https://ref.ly/Deut30:7), [Jb 19:22](https://ref.ly/Job19:22) (כְּמוֺ־אֵל), [Psalm 69:27](https://ref.ly/Ps69:27), [109:16](https://ref.ly/Ps109:16), [119:86](https://ref.ly/Ps119:86), [119:161](https://ref.ly/Ps119:161); c. acc. נַפְשִׁי [143:3](https://ref.ly/Ps143:3), c. acc. נֲדִבָתִי [Jb 30:15](https://ref.ly/Job30:15) (but subj. obscure; read perh. תֵּרָדֵף Bu; Du תִּנָּדֵף); c. ל Pers. [Jb 19:28](https://ref.ly/Job19:28); pt. as subst. [Je 15:15](https://ref.ly/Jer15:15), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Jer17:18), [Psalm 7:2](https://ref.ly/Ps7:2), [31:16](https://ref.ly/Ps31:16) (‖אוֺיְבַי; We מְרַדְּפָי for וַרֹדְפָי), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Ps35:3), [119:84](https://ref.ly/Ps119:84), [119:157](https://ref.ly/Ps119:157) (\+ צָרַי), [142:7](https://ref.ly/Ps142:7). † **g.** *pursue, dog*, subj. דָּם, c. acc. Pers. Ez [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:6) (del va LXX Co Toy Krae); in good sense, *attend closely upon*, טוֺב וָחֶסֶד יִרְדְּפוּנִי [Psalm 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6). † **2\.** fig. *follow after, aim to secure:* in bad sense, c. acc. שַׁלְמֹנִים [Is 1:23](https://ref.ly/Isa1:23), שֵׁכָר [5:11](https://ref.ly/Isa5:11), קָדִים ´ר: [Ho 12:2](https://ref.ly/Hos12:2), רֹדְפֵי זִמַָּה [Psalm 119:150](https://ref.ly/Ps119:150); good sense, c. inf. ´לְדַעַת אֶת־י: [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3), c. acc. צֶדֶק [Dt 16:20](https://ref.ly/Deut16:20), [Is 51:1](https://ref.ly/Isa51:1) (‖ ´מְבַקְשֵׁי י:), צְדָקָה וָחָסֶד [Pr 21:21](https://ref.ly/Prov21:21), שָׁלוֺם [Psalm 34:15](https://ref.ly/Ps34:15) (‖בַּוֵּשׁ), טוֺב [38:21](https://ref.ly/Ps38:21). † **Niph.** pass.: *Pf.* 1 pl. עַל־צַוָּארֵנוּ נִרְדָּפְנוּ [La 5:5](https://ref.ly/Lam5:5) *upon our neck* (i.e. closely) *we are pursued* (improb., cf. Bu); perh. *Impf.* 3 fs. תֵּרָדֵף [Jb 30:15](https://ref.ly/Job30:15) Bu (for תִּרְדֹּף); *Pt.* ´א: יְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת־נִרְדָּף [Ec 3:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:15) *God seeketh the pursued* (i.e. what has disappeared, is past, but dub.). † **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. consec. וְרִדְּפָה [Ho 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hos2:9); *Impf.* 3 ms. יְרַדֶּ־ף [Na 1:8](https://ref.ly/Nah1:8) ([Psalm 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6) v. **Qal**); 3 fs. תְּרַדֵּף [Pr 13:21](https://ref.ly/Prov13:21); *Pt.* מְרֵדֵּ־ף [11:19](https://ref.ly/Prov11:19) \+;—*pursue ardently*, c. acc. Pers. [Ho 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hos2:9) (in fig.); fig. also חשֶׁךְ ´יְר: [Na 1:8](https://ref.ly/Nah1:8) (GunkZAW xiii (1893\), 230, plausibly, יֶהְדֹּף), cf. [Pr 13:31](https://ref.ly/Prov13:31); elsewhere pt., in Pr: *aim (eagerly) to secure,* c. acc. רָעָה [11:19](https://ref.ly/Prov11:19), רֵיקִים [12:11](https://ref.ly/Prov12:11), [28:19](https://ref.ly/Prov28:19), אֲמָרִים [19:7](https://ref.ly/Prov19:7) (mng. dub., v. Toy); in good sense, צְדָקָה [15:9](https://ref.ly/Prov15:9). † **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 ms. consec. וְרֻדַּף כְמֹץ הָרִים [Is 17:13](https://ref.ly/Isa17:13) *it shall be chased away like chaff of the mountains* (‖וְנָס). † **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הִרְּדִיפֻהוּ Ju [20:43](https://ref.ly/Judg20:43) usu. *they chased him* (Benj., coll.), but strange after כִתְּרוּ *surrounded* (still stranger after כתתו or כרתו LXX), and why Hiph.? GFM conj. dittogr. of הִדְרִיכֻהוּ foll.; the reverse conjectured by Bu Now.

## רָהַב (id: BDB08933)

\[Str7292] † \[רָהַב] **vb.** act stormily, boisterously, arrogantly ([Ecclus 13:8](https://ref.ly/Sir13:8), NH *id.,* Hiph. *make proud* (rare), Targum רְהֵב *be arrogant;* As. *ra’âbu, storm* at (angrily); Ar. رَهِبَ *be alarmed, frightened;* Syr. ܪܗܶܒ, Pe. Pt. *trembling, hastening*, Aph. *terrify, hasten*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִרְחֲבוּ הַנַּעַר בַּזָּקֵן [Is 3:5](https://ref.ly/Isa3:5) (‖וְנִגַּש) *storm against* (LXX προςκόψει); *Imv.* ךָ(י)רְהַב רֵעֶ [Pr 6:3](https://ref.ly/Prov6:3) *beset, importune, thy friend* (PerlesAnal. 61 conj. רְהַן *give surety,* cf. NH הרהין). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. sf. הִרְהִיבָֻנִי Ct [6:5](https://ref.ly/Song6:5), of eyes, dub.: *alarm me* Hi Ew Bu, *awe me* Gi, *disturb, confuse me,* Oettli DrIntr.. 419 (446\) Buhl; *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. תַּרְהִבֵנִי [Psalm 138:3](https://ref.ly/Ps138:3) (sibj. ´י:), Buhl Bae Dr *thou makest me proud, bold* (Thes De Che denom. of רֹהַב).

> \[Str7295] † \[רָהָב] **adj.** proud, deflant (si vera l.);—pl. אֶל־רְהָבִים [Psalm 40:5](https://ref.ly/Ps40:5) *unto the proud;* but read prob. הבלים (cf. LXX ματαιότητας).

> \[Str7296] † \[רֹ֫הַב] **n.** \[**m.**] Thes al. **pride,** i.e. obj. of pride;—sf. רָהְבָּם [Psalm 90:10](https://ref.ly/Ps90:10) (cf. ἠ ἀλαζονία τοῦ βίου 1 Jn 2:16); \< LXX Vulgate *their width, extent* (רָחְבָּם).

> \[Str7293–94] † רַ֫הַב **n.** \[**m.**] lit. storm, arrogance, but only as names, v, infr,;—abs. ´ר: [Is 30:7](https://ref.ly/Isa30:7), רָהַב [Jb 9:13](https://ref.ly/Job9:13) \+;—**1\.** mythical sea monster (cf. BartonJAOS xv. 1 (1891\), 22 f.): ´עֹזְרֵי ר: [Jb 9:13](https://ref.ly/Job9:13); יָם [26:12](https://ref.ly/Job26:12) [Psalm 89:11](https://ref.ly/Ps89:11); ‖תַּנִּיו [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9). **2\.** emblemat. name of Egypt, רַהַב וּבָבֶל [Psalm 87:4](https://ref.ly/Ps87:4); ‖מצרים [Is 30:7](https://ref.ly/Isa30:7).

> \[Str4062] מַרְהֵבָה **n.f.** boisterous, raging, behaviour;—[Is 14:4](https://ref.ly/Isa14:4); so read (for MT מַדְהֵבָה) Thes and most, after LXX; ‖נגש (cf. [3:5](https://ref.ly/Isa3:5)).

## I רהט (id: BDB08941)

NöZA xii. 186; Targum רָטַיָּא \= רְהָטִים, [Gn 30:38](https://ref.ly/Gen30:38), [30:41](https://ref.ly/Gen30:41), [Ex 2:16](https://ref.ly/Exod2:16); Syr. ܪܳܗܛܳܐ is appar. *conduit,* also *reservoir* (?); As. *râṭu, vessel* for water, provisions, etc.).

> \[Str7298] † 1\. \[ר֫הַט] **n.** \[**m.**] trough (where water is *collected ?*);—for watering cattle;—pl. רְהָטִים [Gn 30:38](https://ref.ly/Gen30:38), [30:41](https://ref.ly/Gen30:41), [Ex 2:16](https://ref.ly/Exod2:16) (all J).

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 30:41; Exodus 2:16; Genesis 30:38

## רוּד (id: BDB08947)

\[Str7300] † רוּד **vb.** wander restlessly, roam (cf. Ar. رَادَ (و) *go to and fro* \[NöZMG xxxvii (1883\), 539], Eth. ሮደ፡ *run upon, invade, attack*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. עִד רָד עִם אֵל [Ho 12:1](https://ref.ly/Hos12:1), fig., crpt., LXX appar. עַתָּ יְדָעָם, We עִדַּר דַּעַת *lacks knowledge,* Now יָדֻעַ, BewerJBL xxi (1902\), 108 f. עֹד יְדָעָס; 1 pl. רֵדְנוּ [Je 2:31](https://ref.ly/Jer2:31) fig. of Isr.; read וְרַדְתִּי perh. Ju [11:37](https://ref.ly/Judg11:37) lit., v. ירד **1 g. Hiph.** *shew restlessness: Impf.* 2 ms. כַּאֲשֶׁר תָּרִיד [Gn 27:40](https://ref.ly/Gen27:40) (dub. Nöl. c. 540\); 1 s. אָדִיד בְּשִיתִי [Psalm 55:3](https://ref.ly/Ps55:3) *I shew restlessness (?) in my murmuring.*

> \[Str4788] † \[מָרוֺד, Köil. l127 f.] **n.** \[**m.**] restlessness, straying (?);—sf. ניִי וּמְרוּדִי [La 3:19](https://ref.ly/Lam3:19); pl. sf. יְמֵי עָנְיָהּ וּמְרוּדֶיהָ [La 1:7](https://ref.ly/Lam1:7) (rd. prob. מְרוּדָהּ NöZMG xxxvii (1883\), 539\); pl. וַעֲנִיִּם מְרוּדִים [Is 58:7](https://ref.ly/Isa58:7), usu. concr. *the wandering* (homeless) *poor* (Di thinks old Qal pass. Pt.; CheComm. rds. Hoph. מוּרָדִים; BuhlLex Hiph. מָרִידִים; Köii. 1:128 takes MT abstr. for concr. \[appos. of ´וַע:]; so Du CheHpt \[´וַע: gloss], read perhaps pt. רָדִים (מ dittogr.)).

## רָוָה (id: BDB08949)

\[Str7301] † \[רָוָה] **vb.** be saturated, drink one’s fill (NH in der. spec.; Ar. رَوِىَ; Eth. ረወየ፡ Aram. רְוִי, ܪܘܳܐ);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. consec. וְרָוְֽתָּה מִדָּמָם [Je 46:10](https://ref.ly/Jer46:10), fig. of sword (‖שָֽבְעָה, אָֽכְלָה); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִרְוְיֻן מִדֶּשֶׁן בֵּיתֶָךָ [Psalm 36:9](https://ref.ly/Ps36:9) (Ges§ 75 u) fig. of men (‖תַּשְׁקֵם); 1 pl. נִרְוֶה דֹדִים [Pr 7:18](https://ref.ly/Prov7:18) *we will take our fill of love* (‖נִתְעַלְּסָה). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. רִוְּתָה [Is 34:5](https://ref.ly/Isa34:5), ´וְר: consec. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7); 1 s. consec. וְרִוֵּיתִי [Je 31:14](https://ref.ly/Jer31:14); *Impf.* 1 s. sf. אֲרַיָּ֫וֶךָ [Is 16:9](https://ref.ly/Isa16:9) (read prob. אֲרַוַּ֫יִח MargolisAm. J. Sem. Lang., Oct. 1902, 48, vid. also Köi. 589 f.; \> אֲרַוָּ֫יֶח Sta§ 634 e Ges§ 75 dd); 3 mpl. sf. יְרַוֻּךָ [Pr 5:19](https://ref.ly/Prov5:19); *Inf. abs.* רַוֵּה [Psalm 65:11](https://ref.ly/Ps65:11) (Ges§ 113 z);—**1\.** intens. *be intoxicated, drunk* [Is 34:5](https://ref.ly/Isa34:5), fig. of sword; \+ מִדָּם v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7), of land (‖יְדֻשָּׁן). **2\.** causat., *drench, water abundantly*, c. acc. תְּלָמֶיהָ (subj. ´י:); [Is 16:9](https://ref.ly/Isa16:9) c. acc. Pers. \+ דִּמְעָתִי material; *saturate* (fig.), *sate,* וֶפֶשׁ הַכֹּהֲנִים דָּשֶׁן ´ור: [Je 31:14](https://ref.ly/Jer31:14) (‖שבע); sexually, דַּדָּיהָ יְרַוֻּךָ [Pr 5:19](https://ref.ly/Prov5:19). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִרְוָה [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10), sf. הִרְוַנִי [La 3:15](https://ref.ly/Lam3:15); 2 ms. sf. הִרְוִיתָנִי [Is 43:24](https://ref.ly/Isa43:24); 1 s. הִרְוֵיתִי [Je 31:25](https://ref.ly/Jer31:25); *Pt.* מַרְוֶה [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25);—*saturate, water,* c. acc. ארץ [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10) (subj. שֶׁלֶנ, גֶּשֶׁם); *cause to drink* (fig.), c. acc. Pers. etrei (לַעֲנָה) [La 3:15](https://ref.ly/Lam3:15) (‖הִשְבִּיעַנִי); c. acc. Pers. only (נֶפֶשׁ) [Je 31:25](https://ref.ly/Jer31:25) (‖מִלֵּאתִי), abs. [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25); of Isr. satisfying ´י:, חֵלֶב זְבָחִים [Is 43:24](https://ref.ly/Isa43:24). **Hoph.** *Impf.* יוֺרֶא (יָרְוֶה ?) [Pr 11:25](https://ref.ly/Prov11:25) v. Toy and ירא p. 432\.

> \[Str7377] † רִי **n.** \[**m.**] moisture (for \*רְוִי Sta§ 117 c, or \*רִוְיְ Köii. 1, 64; cf. 11\. כִּי);—abs. יַטְרִיחַ עָב [Jb 37:11](https://ref.ly/Job37:11).

[Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12), [Is 58:11](https://ref.ly/Isa58:11); fs. הָרָוָה [Dt 29:18](https://ref.ly/Deut29:18) (opp. הַצְּמֵאָה; appar. of herbage, in proverb, expression for *everything*); perhaps also cstr. רְוֵה [Jb 10:15](https://ref.ly/Job10:15) \= *sated* with afflication (for רְאֵה), v. \[רָאֶה].

> \[Str7310] † רְוָיָה **n.f.** saturation (LagBN 51, 150\);—abs. ´כּוֺסִי ר: [Psalm 23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5) (fig.), i.e. is well\-filled Ges§ 141 c; in gen. ´לָר: [66:12](https://ref.ly/Ps66:12), but Vrss and most mod. לָֽרְוָחָה *to* (a place of) *relief*.

## רוח (id: BDB08954)

רוח (√ of foll.; prob. *breathe, blow* (v. Gerber46); cf. Syr. ܪܳܚ *breathe;* Ar. رَاحَ *be windy;* Eth. ሮሐ፡ *flabello ventilare, ventulum facere;* Ar. رِيحٌ *breath, wind, spirit,* رُوحٌ *soul, spirit;* NH רוּחַ, Aram. רוּחָא, ܪܽܘܚܳܐ *wind, breath, spirit;* also NH רֵיחַ, Aram. רֵיחָא, ܪܺܝܚܳܐ, all *odour;* NH רִיחַ, רוּחַ (usu. Hiph.), Targum Syr. Aph., all *smell*).

> \[Str7307,5315,3824,1320,5315,5315,3820,3820,3820] רוּחַ:378 **n.f.**:[Gn 41:8](https://ref.ly/Gen41:8) (less oft. **m.**:[Ex 10:13](https://ref.ly/Exod10:13) \+) breath, wind, spirit;—abs. ´ר: [Gn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Gen8:1) \+, רָוּחָה [Je 52:23](https://ref.ly/Jer52:23); cstr. רוּחַ [Gn 6:17](https://ref.ly/Gen6:17) \+; sf. רוּחִי v:[3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) \+, רוּחֲךָ [Psalm 104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30) \+, etc.; pl. רוּחוֺת v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps104:4) \+, רֻחוֺת [Je 49:36](https://ref.ly/Jer49:36), רוּחֹת [Nu 16:22](https://ref.ly/Num16:22), [26:16](https://ref.ly/Num26:16) (v. Br:רוח in O.T., JBL xix (1900\), 132 ff., full statement of all passages);—† **1\.** *breath* of mouth or nostrils (33 t.): **a.** רוּחַ פִּיו [Jb 15:30](https://ref.ly/Job15:30) *breath of his mouth,* cf. [19:17](https://ref.ly/Job19:17); אַפֵּינוּ [La 4:20](https://ref.ly/Lam4:20) (fig. of king), of idols לאֹ רוּחַ בָּן [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14) \= [51:17](https://ref.ly/Jer51:17), Hb [2:19](https://ref.ly/Hab2:19), [Psalm 135:17](https://ref.ly/Ps135:17); cf. [Jb 9:18](https://ref.ly/Job9:18). **b.** as mere breath: cf. **2 e.** דִּבְרֵי־רוּחַ [Jb 16:3](https://ref.ly/Job16:3) i.e. windy words; לְרוּחַ אִמְרֵי [Jb 6:26](https://ref.ly/Job6:26), הַנְּבִיאִים יִהְיוּ לְרוּחַ [Je 5:13](https://ref.ly/Jer5:13). **c.** as word of command: (1\) of God: בְּרוּחַ פִּיו כָּ־ל (נעשו) צְבָאָם[Psalm 33:6](https://ref.ly/Ps33:6), cf. [Is 34:16](https://ref.ly/Isa34:16) (‖פֶּה); (2\) of Messianic king: שְפָתָיו יָמִית רָשָׁע [Is 11:4](https://ref.ly/Isa11:4). **d.** as hard breathing through the nostrils in anger: (1\) of God: מֵרוּח אַפּוֺ יִכְלוּ [Jb 4:9](https://ref.ly/Job4:9), [Ex 15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8) (poem), [2 S 22:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:16) \= [Psalm 18:16](https://ref.ly/Ps18:16), [Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28), [59:19](https://ref.ly/Isa59:19); (2\) of man: רוּחַ עָרִיצִים [Is 25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4). **e.** as sign and symbol of life: רוּחַ חַיִּים *breath of life* [Gn 6:17](https://ref.ly/Gen6:17), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Gen7:15) (P); נִשְׁמַת רוּחַ חַיִּים בְּאַפָּיו [Gn 7:22](https://ref.ly/Gen7:22) (P); Ez [37:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:5), cf. v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:6), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:8), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:9), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:9), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:9), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:10), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:14) (*breath* or *spirit*), [Ec 3:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:19). **2\.** *wind* (117 t.): **a.** *wind* of heaven: [Gn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Gen8:1) (P) [Ex 15:10](https://ref.ly/Exod15:10) (E) [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31) (J) [1 K 18:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:45) \+, [Je 10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13), thence [51:16](https://ref.ly/Jer51:16), [Psalm 135:7](https://ref.ly/Ps135:7); ´רוּחַ י: [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15), [Is 40:7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7), cf. [Jb 26:13](https://ref.ly/Job26:13) (Di Bu); † קָדִים(ה)רוּחַ *east wind* [Ex 10:13](https://ref.ly/Exod10:13), [10:13](https://ref.ly/Exod10:13), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Exod14:21) (J) [Psalm 48:8](https://ref.ly/Ps48:8), [Je 18:17](https://ref.ly/Jer18:17), Ez [17:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:10), [19:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:12), [27:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:26), [Jon 4:8](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:8); † צָפוֺן ´ר: *north wind* [Pr 25:23](https://ref.ly/Prov25:23); † יָם ´ר: *sea wind* (west wind) [Ex 10:19](https://ref.ly/Exod10:19) (J); †. הַיּוֺם ´ר: *day wind*, evening wind [Gn 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8) (J; cf. Ct [2:17](https://ref.ly/Song2:17), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Song4:6)); † אַרְבַּע רוּחוֺת *four winds* [Je 49:36](https://ref.ly/Jer49:36), Ez [37:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:9) (quarters?) [Dn 8:8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Dan11:4) (fig. quarters; cf. **b**), [Zc 2:10](https://ref.ly/Zech2:10), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Zech6:5); רוּהַּ סְעְרָה *storm wind* [Psalm 107:25](https://ref.ly/Ps107:25), [148:8](https://ref.ly/Ps148:8), Ez [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:4) (סְעָרוֺת ´ר:), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:11), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:13); רוּחַ סֹעָה *rushing wind* [Psalm 55:9](https://ref.ly/Ps55:9) (read poss. סערה Hup); בַּעְיָם רוּחוֺ [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15) (read ´בְּעֹצֶם ר:, v. עֲיָם, עֹצֶם), etc; wind personif.: כַּנְפֵי רוּחַ [2 S 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:11) \= [Psalm 18:11](https://ref.ly/Ps18:11), [104:3](https://ref.ly/Ps104:3), cf. [Ho 4:19](https://ref.ly/Hos4:19), [Psalm 104:4](https://ref.ly/Ps104:4). **b.** *quarter* (of wind), *side:* רוּחַ הַקָּדִים Ez [42:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:16) *east side;* רוּחַ הַצָּפוֺן v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:17) *north side;* רוּחַ הַדָּרוֺם v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:18) *south side;* רוּחַ הַיָּם v:[19](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:19) *west side;* אַרְבַּע רוּחוֺת v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:20), [1 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:24) *four sides;* רָוּחָה [Je 52:23](https://ref.ly/Jer52:23) *on the sides*. **c.** *breath of air:* שָׁאֲפוּ רוּחַ כַּתַּנִּים [Je 14:6](https://ref.ly/Jer14:6); רוּחַ אֹ יָבֹּא בֵּינֵיהֶם [Jb 41:8](https://ref.ly/Job41:8). **d.** *air, gas,* from womb (dub.): כְּמוֺ יָלַדְנוּ רוּחַ [Is 26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18) (Di, **e**); רוּחֲכֶם אֵשׁ תֹּאכַלְכֶם (RV breath) [Is 33:11](https://ref.ly/Isa33:11) (Du, **3 c**). **e.** *vain, empty thing:* רוּחַיָּ\+י [Jb 7:7](https://ref.ly/Job7:7) *my life is wind;* דַּעַת רוּחַ [15:2](https://ref.ly/Job15:2); רוּחַ וָתֹהוּ נִסְכֵּיהֶם [Is 41:29](https://ref.ly/Isa41:29); רְעוּת רוּחַ *striving for wind* [Ec 1:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:14), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:11), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:17), [2:26](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:26), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:4), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:6), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:9); cf. [1:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:17), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:16), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:15). † **3\.** *spirit,* as that which breathes quickly *in animation or agitation \= temper, disposition* (76 t.; so, distinctively, as compared with נֶפֶשׁ and לֵבָב): **a.** *spirit, animation, vivacity, vigour:* לאֹ הָיָה בָהּ עוֺד רוּחַ [1 K 10:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:5) \= [2 Ch 9:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:4); מַה־זֶּה רוּחֲךָ סָרָה [1 K 21:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:5); יַתְּחִי רוּחַ [Gn 45:27](https://ref.ly/Gen45:27) (E); וַתָּשָׁב רוּחוֺ Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19), [1 S 30:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:12). **b.** *courage:* ´לאֹ הָיָה בָם עוֺד ר: [Jos 5:1](https://ref.ly/Josh5:1); נְגִידִים ´יִבְצֹר ר: [Psalm 76:13](https://ref.ly/Ps76:13); מִצְרַיִם ´וְנָֽבְקָה ר: [Is 19:3](https://ref.ly/Isa19:3); תתעטּף רוּחִי [Psalm 77:4](https://ref.ly/Ps77:4), [142:4](https://ref.ly/Ps142:4), [143:4](https://ref.ly/Ps143:4); לאֹ קָמָה ´עוֺד ר: [Jos 2:11](https://ref.ly/Josh2:11); יְכַלְכֵּל מַחֲלֵהוּ [Pr 18:14](https://ref.ly/Prov18:14). **c.** *temper,* esp. *anger:* ´רוּחַ רָעָה בֵּין וגו: Ju [9:23](https://ref.ly/Judg9:23); אָז רָֽפְתָה רוּחָם [8:3](https://ref.ly/Judg8:3); משֵׁל בְּרוּחוֺ [Pr 16:32](https://ref.ly/Prov16:32), cf. [25:28](https://ref.ly/Prov25:28), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Prov29:11), [Jb 15:13](https://ref.ly/Job15:13); הֵנִיחוּ אֶת־רוּחִי [Zc 6:8](https://ref.ly/Zech6:8); [Ec 7:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:9), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:4). **d.** *impatience* or *patience:* קֹצֶר רוּחַ [Ex 6:9](https://ref.ly/Exod6:9) (P) *impatience,* hastiness of temper, cf. (of ´י:) [Mi 2:7](https://ref.ly/Mic2:7); קְצַר־רוּחַ [Pr 14:29](https://ref.ly/Prov14:29) (‖אֶרֶצ אַפַיִם); הֱצִיקַתְנִי רוּחַ בִּטְנִי [Jb 32:18](https://ref.ly/Job32:18) (Du *breath;* Di Bu *divine spirit,* cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Job32:8)); אִם מַדּוּעַ לאֹ־תִקְצַר רוּחִי [Jb 21:4](https://ref.ly/Job21:4); ´הֲקָצַר רוּחַ י: [Mi 2:7](https://ref.ly/Mic2:7); ´אֶרֶצ ר: [Ec 7:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:8). **e.** *spirit, disposition,* as troubled, bitter, or discontented; (ו)וַתִּפָּעֶם רוּח (*his*) *spirit was troubled* [Gn 41:8](https://ref.ly/Gen41:8) (E) [Dn 2:3](https://ref.ly/Dan2:3), cf. v:[1](https://ref.ly/Dan2:1); מֹרַת רוּחַ [Gn 26:35](https://ref.ly/Gen26:35) (P) *bitterness of spirit;* ´עֲצוּבַת ר: [Is 54:6](https://ref.ly/Isa54:6), cf. Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14), [Jb 6:4](https://ref.ly/Job6:4). **f.** as crushed: וְכִהֲתָה כָל־רוּחַ Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12), cf. [Is 61:3](https://ref.ly/Isa61:3), [Pr 15:4](https://ref.ly/Prov15:4), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Prov15:13), [17:22](https://ref.ly/Prov17:22), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Prov18:14), [Psalm 143:7](https://ref.ly/Ps143:7). **g.** *disposition* of various kinds, oft. *unaccountable and uncontrollable impulse:* הֵעִיר אֶת־רוּחַ [1 Ch 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:26), [5:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:26), [2 Ch 21:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:16), [36:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:22), [Ezr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:1), [1:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:5), [Je 51:11](https://ref.ly/Jer51:11), [Hg 1:14](https://ref.ly/Hag1:14), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hag1:14), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hag1:14); הִנְנִי תֵן בּוֺ רוּח(וֺ)נ[2 K 19:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:7) \= [Is 37:7](https://ref.ly/Isa37:7); [Nu 14:24](https://ref.ly/Num14:24) (J; v. עִם **4 b**), [Mal 2:15](https://ref.ly/Mal2:15), [2:15](https://ref.ly/Mal2:15), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Mal2:16), [Dt 2:30](https://ref.ly/Deut2:30); רוּחַ קִנְאָה *jealous disposition* [Nu 5:14](https://ref.ly/Num5:14), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Num5:14), [5:30](https://ref.ly/Num5:30) (P); וְנוּנִים [Ho 4:12](https://ref.ly/Hos4:12), [5:4](https://ref.ly/Hos5:4); רוּחַ עִוְעִים[Is 19:14](https://ref.ly/Isa19:14); רוּחַ מִשְׁפָּט [28:6](https://ref.ly/Isa28:6); אִישׁ אֲשֶׁר רוּחַ אֱלהִֹרם בּוֺ(administrative) [Gn 41:38](https://ref.ly/Gen41:38) (E); קַר רוּחַ אִישׁ הְּבוּנָה [Pr 17:27](https://ref.ly/Prov17:27). **h.** *prophetic spirit:* יְהוֺשֻׁעַ אִישׁ אֲשֶׁ־ר רוּחַ בּוּ [Nu 27:18](https://ref.ly/Num27:18) (P; or **g,** as [Gn 41:38](https://ref.ly/Gen41:38)); רוּחַ אֵלִיָּהוּ עַל־אֱלִישָׁע[2 K 2:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:15), cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9); רוּחַ תַּרְדֵמָה *spirit of deep sleep* (ecstatic, cf. [Gn 2:21](https://ref.ly/Gen2:21), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Gen15:12), but Di al. **g,** as [Is 19:14](https://ref.ly/Isa19:14)) [Is 29:10](https://ref.ly/Isa29:10); [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11); רוּחַ הַטֻמְאָה [Zc 13:2](https://ref.ly/Zech13:2) (of lying proph.), cf. Ez [13:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:3). † **4\.** *spirit* of the living, breathing being, dwelling in the בָּשָר of men and animals, ‖נֶפֶשׁ (25 t.): **a.** gift and creation of God: יֹאֵר רוּחַ אָדָם בְּקִרְבּוֺ [Zc 12:1](https://ref.ly/Zech12:1); רוּחַ אֱלוֺהַּ בְּאַפִּי [Jb 27:3](https://ref.ly/Job27:3), cf. [Is 42:5](https://ref.ly/Isa42:5). **b.** God preserves it: פְּקֻדָּתְךָ שָֽׁמְרָה רוּחִי [Jb 10:12](https://ref.ly/Job10:12), cf. [12:10](https://ref.ly/Job12:10); אֱלֽהֵי הָרוּחֹת לְכָל בָּשָר [Nu 16:22](https://ref.ly/Num16:22), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Num27:16) (P); תֹּכֵן רוּחוֺת [Pr 16:2](https://ref.ly/Prov16:2). **c.** it is therefore God’s spirit: [Gn 6:3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) (J; v. דִּין ad fin.). **d.** it departs at death: [Psalm 78:39](https://ref.ly/Ps78:39) (Dr *a wind* that passeth *away* **2 e**); חַיֵּי רוּחִי [Is 38:16](https://ref.ly/Isa38:16) (Di *principle of life*), [Psalm 146:4](https://ref.ly/Ps146:4); esp. [104:29](https://ref.ly/Ps104:29), [104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30), [Jb 17:1](https://ref.ly/Job17:1), [34:14](https://ref.ly/Job34:14) (cf. v:[15](https://ref.ly/Job34:15)), [Is 57:16](https://ref.ly/Isa57:16), [Ec 8:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:8), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:8) (*wind* Wild); אֵינְךָ יוֺדֵעַ מַה־דֶּרֶח הָרוּחַ [Ec 11:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:5), cf. [3:21](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:21); over ag. הָרוּחַ תָּשׁוּב אֶל־הָאֱלֽהִים אֲשֶׁר נְתָנָהּ [12:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:7); יְָֽדְךָ אַפְקִיד רוּחִי [Psalm 31:6](https://ref.ly/Ps31:6). **e.** disembodied being (dub., Di Du *breath of wind*): וְרוּחַ עַל־פָּנַי יַחֲלֽף [Jb 4:15](https://ref.ly/Job4:15). † **5\.** *spirit* as seat of emotion \= נֶפֶשׁ: **a.** *desire* (poss. **3 g**), [Is 26:9](https://ref.ly/Isa26:9) (‖נֶפֶשׁ). **b.** *sorrow, trouble* (prob. **3 e**), [Jb 7:11](https://ref.ly/Job7:11) (‖נֶפֶשׁ).—[1 S 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:15) v. קָשֶׁה. † **6\.** occasionally, (and late) \= seat or organ of mental acts, ‖לֵב, or synon. with it: רוּחַ חָכְמָה [Ex 28:3](https://ref.ly/Exod28:3), [Dt 34:9](https://ref.ly/Deut34:9) (both P; prob. **3 g**); תּעֵי רוּחַ [Is 29:24](https://ref.ly/Isa29:24), cf. [Jb 20:3](https://ref.ly/Job20:3) (Hi Bu Du *wind* of Job’s words), [Psalm 77:7](https://ref.ly/Ps77:7) (LXX Sym SyrVer Jer troubled *disposition*); רוּחַ יהוה [Is 40:13](https://ref.ly/Isa40:13); come into *mind* Ez [11:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:5), [20:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:32) (cf. לֵב [Is 65:17](https://ref.ly/Isa65:17), [Je 3:16](https://ref.ly/Jer3:16) \+); also \= לַב רוּחַ חֲדָשָׁהEz [11:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:19), [18:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:31), [36:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:26); רוּחִי v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:27), [Is 59:21](https://ref.ly/Isa59:21) (רוּחִי, ‖דְּבָרַי; but prob. prophetic spirit **9 b**); חָדָשׁ Ez [18:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:31), [36:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:26); נְכֵה רוּחַ [Is 66:2](https://ref.ly/Isa66:2); דַּכְּאֵי רוּחַ [Psalm 34:19](https://ref.ly/Ps34:19) (cf. לֵב נִדְכָּאִים [Is 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15)); מִשֵּׁבֶר רוּחַ [Is 65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14) (cf. **3 f**); רוּחַ נִשְׁבָּרֶה [Psalm 51:19](https://ref.ly/Ps51:19) (‖לֵב נִשְׁבָּר, cf. נִשְׁבְּרֵי לֵב [34:19](https://ref.ly/Ps34:19), [Is 61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1)); [Psalm 32:2](https://ref.ly/Ps32:2), [Pr 11:13](https://ref.ly/Prov11:13), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Prov16:18), [Psalm 78:8](https://ref.ly/Ps78:8), [Ec 7:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:8) (cf. גְּבַהּלֵב [Pr 16:5](https://ref.ly/Prov16:5)); not D or Je or any Deut. writer; conception of its activity in inspiring prophecy prob. discredited from abuse by false prophets, v. נביא, נבא): **a.** as inspiring ecstatic state of prophecy, [Nu 11:17](https://ref.ly/Num11:17), [11:25](https://ref.ly/Num11:25), [11:25](https://ref.ly/Num11:25), [11:26](https://ref.ly/Num11:26), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Num11:29) (J), [1 S 10:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:6), [10:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:10) (cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:5)), [19:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:20), [19:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:23); cf. also (in earlier prophets) אִישׁ הָרוּחַ, i.e. one possessed by the spirit in the ecstatic state, ‖הַנָּבִּיא [Ho 9:7](https://ref.ly/Hos9:7); ´רוּחַ י: [Mi 3:8](https://ref.ly/Mic3:8) (‖בֹּחַ) is prob. gloss (We Now); וְלאֹ רוּחִי [Is 30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1); רוּחַ dealing with Ezek.: Ez [2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:2), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:12), [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14), [3:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:24), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:3), [11:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:1), [11:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:5), [11:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:24) (Co gloss), [37:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:1), [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:5) (all implying ecstatic state of vision), cf. Elijah [1 K 18:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:12), [2 K 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:16). **b.** *spirit* as impelling proph. to utter instruction or warning (higher and later conception): transition prob. [Nu 24:2](https://ref.ly/Num24:2), [2 S 23:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:2), [1 Ch 12:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:18); elsewhere in Ch.: [2 Ch 15:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:1), [20:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:14), [24:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:20); distinctly in Is:2, [48:16](https://ref.ly/Isa48:16), cf. [61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1); so of ancient prophets, [Zc 7:12](https://ref.ly/Zech7:12), [Ne 9:30](https://ref.ly/Neh9:30), cf., of future prophetic gift, [Jo 3:1](https://ref.ly/John3:1), [3:2](https://ref.ly/John3:2). **c.** imparting warlike energy, and executive and administrative power: (1\) to שֹׁפְטִים, מוֺשִׁיעִים, מְלָכִים, of ancient Isr.: וַתְּהִי רוּחַ על ´י: Ju [3:10](https://ref.ly/Judg3:10), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Judg11:29), cf. [6:34](https://ref.ly/Judg6:34), [13:25](https://ref.ly/Judg13:25), [14:6](https://ref.ly/Judg14:6), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Judg14:19), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14), [1 S 11:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:6), [16:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:13), [16:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:14); so also יְעָרֶה עָלֵינוּ רוּחַ מִמָּרוֺם [Is 32:15](https://ref.ly/Isa32:15); (2\) resting upon Messianic king: [Is 11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2); upon servant of ´י:, [42:1](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1). **d.** late, as endowing men with various gifts: technical skill [Ex 31:3](https://ref.ly/Exod31:3), [35:31](https://ref.ly/Exod35:31) (P); understanding [Jb 32:8](https://ref.ly/Job32:8) (‖נִשְׁמַת שַׁדַּי); poured out by divine wisdom [Pr 1:23](https://ref.ly/Prov1:23). **e.** as energy of life: רוּחַ אֱלֽהִים מְרַחֶפֶת עַל־פְּנֵי הַמָּיִם [Gn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Gen1:2) (P); רוּחַ אֵל עָשָתְנִי [Jb 33:4](https://ref.ly/Job33:4) (‖נִשְׁמַת שַׁדַּי); as vital power, opp. בָשָר: [Is 31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3); in cherubic chariot: Ez [1:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:12), cf. v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:20), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:21), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:17); reviving Israel Ez [39:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:29), [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10), [Is 44:3](https://ref.ly/Isa44:3). **f.** \= ancient angel of the presence and later Shekina: קָדְשׁוֺ ´ר: [Is 63:10](https://ref.ly/Isa63:10), [63:11](https://ref.ly/Isa63:11) \= ´י :´ר: v:[14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14) (\= מַלְאַךְ פָּנָיו v:[9](https://ref.ly/Isa63:9)), cf. [Psalm 106:33](https://ref.ly/Ps106:33); so also קָדְשְׁךָ ´ר: [Psalm 51:13](https://ref.ly/Ps51:13) (in national prayer), cf. [Ne 9:20](https://ref.ly/Neh9:20), [Psalm 143:10](https://ref.ly/Ps143:10); proph. of restoration conceive of the divine spirit as standing in their midst and about to fulfil all divine promises: רוּחִי עֹמֶדֶת בְּתוֺכֲכֶם [Hg 2:5](https://ref.ly/Hag2:5), [Zc 4:6](https://ref.ly/Zech4:6); this conception culminates in רוּחַ \= divine Presence, and as such omnipresent, [Psalm 139:7](https://ref.ly/Ps139:7) (‖פָּנֶיךָ; cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ps139:8)).

## רָרַח (id: BDB08958)

\[Str7304] † רָרַח **vb.** be wide, spacious (NH *id., extend* (intrans.), רֶוַח *wide space;* Ar. رَوِحَ *be wide* (between thighs), and deriv.; cf. perhaps Eth. ረኀወ፡ *make open, open* (der. spec.; DI293); Aram. רְוַח *be wide* (usu. fig. as Heb.), ܪܳܚ, ܪܘܰܚ *be wide, enlarged*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ר:, sq. ל Pers. [1 S 16:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:23) fig. there *was enlargement, relief,* for Saul; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְוַח לִי [Jb 32:20](https://ref.ly/Job32:20) that *there may be relief* for me. **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְרֻוָּחִים [Je 22:14](https://ref.ly/Jer22:14) *spacious* (of rooms; ‖בֵּות מִדּוֺת).

> \[Str7305] † רֶ֫וַח **n. m. 1\.** space, interval [Gn 32:17](https://ref.ly/Gen32:17) (E; וּבֵין…בֵּין:). **2\.** respite, relief, [Est 4:14](https://ref.ly/Esth4:14).

> \[Str7309] † רְוָחָה **n.f.** respite, relief;—abs. ´ר: [Ex 8:11](https://ref.ly/Exod8:11) (J); sf. רַוְחָתִי [La 3:56](https://ref.ly/Lam3:56) (Ew Löhr BuComm. צַוְחָתִי *my outcry;* then del. שַׁוְעָתִי as gloss); rd. לָֽרְוָחָה also for MT לָֽרְוָיָה [Psalm 66:12](https://ref.ly/Ps66:12) (v. sub רוה).

> \[Str7310] רְוָיָה v. רוה

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Samuel 16:23; Jeremiah 22:14; Job 32:20; Psalms 66:12; Genesis 32:17; Exodus 8:11; Esther 4:14; Lamentations 3:56
* **Associated Articles:** רָוָה (ID: `BDB08949@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## רוּם (id: BDB08962)

\[Str7311,7213,7319] † רוּם **vb.** be high, exalted, rise (OHeb., Ph., OAram., Palm., all in n. pr.; Sab. in רים epith. dei et regis FellZMG liv (1900\), 25 f. also in n. pr. Mordtmib. xxx (1876\), 36 SabDenkm No. 14, 1\. 1; Ar. رام *be high,* dial. of Oman (JayakarJA xxi. 813, 875\) and Zanzibar (PräZMG xxxiv (1880\), 218\); Eth. in deriv.; Aram. רָם, ܪܘܡ (very often in der. spec. and deriv.));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. consec. וְרָם [Dt 8:14](https://ref.ly/Deut8:14) \+; 3 fs. רָ֫מָה [1 S 2:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:1) \+; וְרָאֲמָה [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10) v. רָאַם; 3 pl. רָמוּ [Pr 30:13](https://ref.ly/Prov30:13), [Psalm 131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1), רָמּוּ [Jb 22:12](https://ref.ly/Job22:12) (Baer Gi; Ges: § 20 i); *Impf.* יָרוּרָם [Is 30:18](https://ref.ly/Isa30:18) \+, juss. יָרֹם [Nu 24:7](https://ref.ly/Num24:7), וַיָּרָם [Ho 13:6](https://ref.ly/Hos13:6), Ez [10:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:4) \[וֵיָּרֻם [Ex 16:20](https://ref.ly/Exod16:20) v. רמם]; 3 mpl. יְרֻמָוּן [Is 49:11](https://ref.ly/Isa49:11), etc.; *Imv.* ms. ר֫וּמָה [Psalm 21:4](https://ref.ly/Ps21:4) \+; *Inf. cstr.* רוּם [Dt 17:20](https://ref.ly/Deut17:20), Ez [10:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:16), כְּרֻם [Psalm 12:9](https://ref.ly/Ps12:9) \[Bae conj. כֶּרֶם], sf. רוֺמָם Ez [10:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:17) (Ges: § 72 q); *Pt.* רָם [Is 6:1](https://ref.ly/Isa6:1) \+, pl. רָמִים [2:13](https://ref.ly/Isa2:13) \+, cstr. רָמֵי [10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33), etc, etc.;—**1\. a.** *be high,* lit. rock (in fig.) [Psalm 61:3](https://ref.ly/Ps61:3) (מִן comp.); *be (set on) high,* רָמּוּ [Jb 22:12](https://ref.ly/Job22:12) (of stars), esp. pt., \= adj., in gen. [Is 2:12](https://ref.ly/Isa2:12) (\+ גֵּאֶה), of mts. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Isa2:14), [Dt 12:2](https://ref.ly/Deut12:2), hill Ez [6:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:13), [20:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:28), [34:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:6), throne [Is 6:1](https://ref.ly/Isa6:1), trees \= *tall* [2:13](https://ref.ly/Isa2:13), Ez [17:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:22), cf. (in fig.) [Is 10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33) (רָמֵי הַקוֺמָה); human stature [Dt 1:28](https://ref.ly/Deut1:28) (מִן comp.), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Deut2:10), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Deut2:21), [9:2](https://ref.ly/Deut9:2); רָמִים [Psalm 78:69](https://ref.ly/Ps78:69) *heights* (of heaven; ‖אֶרֶץ); רָמִים of inhab. of heavens [Jb 21:22](https://ref.ly/Job21:22). **b.** esp. of ´י:, וְנִשָא [Is 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15), cf. [Psalm 138:6](https://ref.ly/Ps138:6), \+ עַל־כָּל־הָעַמִּים [99:2](https://ref.ly/Ps99:2), גּוֺיִם [113:4](https://ref.ly/Ps113:4). **2\.** *be raised, uplifted:* **a.** of highway [Is 49:11](https://ref.ly/Isa49:11) (*made high*, put in order); voice, [Dt 27:14](https://ref.ly/Deut27:14) (pt. \= adj.) *uplifted.* **b.** fig.: of hand, symbol of might, [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27), \+ עַל Pers. [Mi 5:8](https://ref.ly/Mic5:8); of Isr. in Exodus בְּיָד רָמָה [Ex 14:8](https://ref.ly/Exod14:8), [Nu 33:3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3) (both P); of ´י:’s hand [Is 26:11](https://ref.ly/Isa26:11), [Psalm 89:14](https://ref.ly/Ps89:14) (‖תָּעֹז); fig. of presumption [Nu 15:30](https://ref.ly/Num15:30) (P); זְרוֺעַ רָמָה of might [Jb 38:15](https://ref.ly/Job38:15); of eyes, fig. of arrogance [Pr 6:17](https://ref.ly/Prov6:17), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Prov30:13), [Psalm 131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1) (‖גָּבַהּ לִבִּי), so [18:28](https://ref.ly/Ps18:28), but ‖ [2 S 22:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:28) of Pers.; of heart, fig. of reckless elation, [Ho 13:6](https://ref.ly/Hos13:6), [Dt 8:14](https://ref.ly/Deut8:14), Ez [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:10) (בְּגָבְהוֺ, בְּקוֺמָה גָּבַהְתָּ) [Dn 11:12](https://ref.ly/Dan11:12), \+ מִן Pers. [Dt 17:20](https://ref.ly/Deut17:20); of horn, fig. of triumph [1 S 2:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:1) (song), [Psalm 89:25](https://ref.ly/Ps89:25) (v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ps89:18) see **Hiph.**), [112:9](https://ref.ly/Ps112:9), so head [27:6](https://ref.ly/Ps27:6) (\+ עַל Pers.), [140:9](https://ref.ly/Ps140:9) (join ירומו to v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ps89:10), but read יָרִימוּ Che, cf. Hup\-Now). **c.** of Pers., *be exalted* (in fig.), king [Nu 24:7](https://ref.ly/Num24:7) (JE; \+ מִן comp.; ‖תִּנַּשֵּא); of God (´י:, i.e. shew his exaltation) [Is 30:18](https://ref.ly/Isa30:18), [2 S 22:47](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:47) \= [Psalm 18:47](https://ref.ly/Ps18:47), [Psalm 21:14](https://ref.ly/Ps21:14), [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ps46:11); [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ps46:11), \+ שָּׁמַיִם(הַ)עַ־ל [57:6](https://ref.ly/Ps57:6), [57:12](https://ref.ly/Ps57:12), [108:6](https://ref.ly/Ps108:6); ´עֶבֶד י: [Is 52:13](https://ref.ly/Isa52:13), ´י:’s people [Psalm 89:17](https://ref.ly/Ps89:17); rebellious [66:7](https://ref.ly/Ps66:7) (Qr; Kt Hiph.: *shew exaltation*); worthlessness (personif.) [Psalm 12:9](https://ref.ly/Ps12:9); of city [Pr 11:11](https://ref.ly/Prov11:11). **3\.** *be lifted, rise,* of ark, מֵעַל־הארץ [Gn 7:17](https://ref.ly/Gen7:17) (J), so of cherubim Ez [10:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:16), cf. v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:17); ´כְּבוֺד י: v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:4) (מֵעַל הַכְּרוּב).—[Pr 24:7](https://ref.ly/Prov24:7) v. I. רָאמוֺת p. 910 b. **Pō‘lēl** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. רֹמְמָתְהוּ Ez [31:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:4); 1 s. רוֺמַמְתִּי [Is 1:2](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4); *Impf.* 3 ms. יְרוֺמֵם [Ho 11:7](https://ref.ly/Hos11:7), sf. יְרוֺמְמֵנִי [Psalm 27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5), וִירוֺמִמְךָ [37:34](https://ref.ly/Ps37:34); 1 s. sf. וַאֲרֹמְמֶ֫נְהוּ [Ex 15:2](https://ref.ly/Exod15:2) (Ges§ 58 i), etc. (אֵרוֺמָם v. **Hithpō‘l.**); *Imv.* mpl. רוֺמְמוּ [Psalm 99:5](https://ref.ly/Ps99:5), [99:9](https://ref.ly/Ps99:9); *Inf. cstr.* לְרוֺמֵם [Ezr 9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:9); *Pt.* מְרוֺמֵם [1 S 2:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:7), sf. מְרוֺמְמִי [Psalm 9:14](https://ref.ly/Ps9:14), f. רוֺמֵמָה [Psalm 118:16](https://ref.ly/Ps118:16) (מ om., Hup\-Now Bae; cf. Köi. 454\);—**1\. a.** *raise, rear,* children [Is 1:2](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4) (both ‖גִּדֵּל). **b.** *cause* tree *to grow,* subj. תְּהוֺם Ez [31:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:4) (‖גִּדֵּל). **c.** *rear, erect,* temple [Ezr 9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:9). **2\.** *lift up:* **a.** in fig., acc. Pers., subj. ´י: [Psalm 27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5) (\+ בְּצוּר); \+ מִן comp. [2 S 22:49](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:49) \= [Psalm 18:49](https://ref.ly/Ps18:49), \+ מִן *from* [9:14](https://ref.ly/Ps9:14); acc. om. [Ho 11:7](https://ref.ly/Hos11:7) (si vera l., cf. (for sacrif.), [2 Ch 30:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:24), [30:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:24), [35:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:7), [35:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:8) (acc. om.), v:[9](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:9). **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוּרָם [Ex 29:27](https://ref.ly/Exod29:27), הֻרַם [Dn 8:11](https://ref.ly/Dan8:11) Qr (Kt **Hiph.**); *Impf.* 3 ms. יוּרָם [Lv 4:10](https://ref.ly/Lev4:10);—*be taken off* from (מִן) [Lv 4:10](https://ref.ly/Lev4:10) (P), cf. > \[Str7312] † רוּם, רֻם **n.\[m.]** height, haughtiness (prop. inf.);—abs. ´ר:, **1\.** *height, loftiness,* שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ לָעֹמֶק ´לָר: [Pr 25:3](https://ref.ly/Prov25:3); cstr. רוּם עֵינַיִם fig. of *haughtiness* [Is 10:12](https://ref.ly/Isa10:12) (‖גֹּדֶל לֵבָב), [Pr 21:4](https://ref.ly/Prov21:4) (‖רְחַב־לֵב); so רֻם לִבּוֺ [Je 48:29](https://ref.ly/Jer48:29) (‖גַּאֲוָה, גָּאוֺן, גֹּבַהּ); then alone: **2\.** *haughtiness,* רוּם אֲנָשִׁים [Is 2:11](https://ref.ly/Isa2:11), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Isa2:17) (both ‖גַּבְהוּת).

rd. perh. תְּמוּנָה LXX (ὁμοίωμα v:[19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19)) Du; other conj. in CheHpt, v. also III. \[סָכַן].—Vbs. c. ´ת: are: הֵרִים Ez [45:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:1) \+ 14 t., הֵבִיא [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6) \+ 9 t., נָתַן Ez [44:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:30) \+ 9 t., לָקַח [Ex 25:2](https://ref.ly/Exod25:2), [25:2](https://ref.ly/Exod25:2) \+ 4 t., הִקְרִיב [Lv 7:14](https://ref.ly/Lev7:14), [Nu 5:9](https://ref.ly/Num5:9).—Vid. ## רוּן (id: BDB08982)

\[Str7442,7442,7323,7321] † \[רוּן] **vb.** overcome (cf. Ar. رَانَ (ي), c. على or ب Pers., *overcome,* e.g. of wine);—assumed in Heb. by Thes (after LXX) and most mod. to explain **Hithpō‘.** *Pt.* כְּגִבּוֺר מִתְרוֺנֵן מִָ\+יּיִן [Psalm 78:65](https://ref.ly/Ps78:65) *like a hero overcome by wine* (cf. ‖ va); AV RV De Che al. *shout* (√רנן), cf. Targum; so Hup\-Now, with altern. conj. מִתְעוֺרֵר, and this sense more suitable (cf. LuzzattoIl Pentateuco iii (1874\), 162 ([Lv 21:7](https://ref.ly/Lev21:7)) Perles79).—[Pr 29:6](https://ref.ly/Prov29:6) v. רנן, רוץ. p. 930, 943

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 29:6; Psalms 78:65; Leviticus 21:7
* **Associated Articles:** רָנַן (ID: `BDB09150@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## רוּעַ (id: BDB08983)

\[Str7321] † \[רוּעַ] **vb. Hiph.**:40 **1\.** raise a shout. **2\.** give a blast with clarion or horn (NH *id.,* Hiph.; Thes cp. Ar. رَغَا *utter a grumbling cry*);—*Pf.* 3 mpl. הֵרִיעוּ Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14), [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11), הֵרֵעוּ [1 S 17:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:20), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יָרִיעַ [Is 42:13](https://ref.ly/Isa42:13), [Psalm 41:12](https://ref.ly/Ps41:12), וַיָּ֫רַע [Jos 6:20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20), etc.; *Imv.* fs. הָרִיעִי [Zc 9:9](https://ref.ly/Zech9:9); mpl. הָרִיעוּ [Jos 6:10](https://ref.ly/Josh6:10) \+; *Inf. cstr.* הָרִיעַ [2 Ch 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:12), [13:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:15); *Pt.* pl. מְרִיעִים [Ezr 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13);—**1\.** *shout a war\-cry,* or *alarm of battle,* [Jos 6:10](https://ref.ly/Josh6:10), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Josh6:10), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Josh6:10), [6:16](https://ref.ly/Josh6:16), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20), Ju [7:21](https://ref.ly/Judg7:21), [1 S 17:52](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:52), [Is 42:13](https://ref.ly/Isa42:13), [2 Ch 13:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:15), [13:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:15); הריע תְּרוּעָה [Jos 6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20); לקראת Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14); בַּמִּלְחָמָה [1 S 17:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:20). **2\.** *sound a signal for war* or *march:* c. חֲצֹצְרוֺת [Nu 10:7](https://ref.ly/Num10:7), [10:9](https://ref.ly/Num10:9) (P); ‖תקע שׁוֺפָר [Ho 5:8](https://ref.ly/Hos5:8), cf. [Jo 2:1](https://ref.ly/John2:1); c. על *against* [2 Ch 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:12); so also prob. [Jb 30:5](https://ref.ly/Job30:5) (against a thief). **3\.** *shout in triumph* over enemies: c. עַל, [Je 50:15](https://ref.ly/Jer50:15), [Psalm 41:12](https://ref.ly/Ps41:12); abs. [Zp 3:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:14) (‖רנן). **4\.** *shout in applause:* abs. [1 S 10:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:24), [Zc 9:9](https://ref.ly/Zech9:9), cf. (of angels) [Jb 38:7](https://ref.ly/Job38:7). **5\.** *shout* with religious impulse: תְּרוּעָה ´הר: [1 S 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:5), [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13); so in public worship with music and sacrifice, c. לְ, to God [Psalm 47:2](https://ref.ly/Ps47:2), [66:1](https://ref.ly/Ps66:1), [81:2](https://ref.ly/Ps81:2), [95:1](https://ref.ly/Ps95:1), [95:2](https://ref.ly/Ps95:2), [98:4](https://ref.ly/Ps98:4), [100:1](https://ref.ly/Ps100:1), לפני [98:6](https://ref.ly/Ps98:6); in joy, תַּחְתִּיּוֺת אֶרֶץ [Is 44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23) (‖תָנּוּ שָׁמַיִם). **6\.** *cry out in distress,* [Is 15:4](https://ref.ly/Isa15:4) (but Du Bu יָֽרְעוּ *tremble*); לָמָּה תָרִיעִי רֵָעַ [Mi 4:9](https://ref.ly/Mic4:9) (of Zion under fig. of woman, but perh. read למה תָּרוֺעִי רֹעַ *why art thou broken all to pieces?* as [Pr 11:15](https://ref.ly/Prov11:15)). **Pō‘lal.** *Impf.* 3 ms. impers. לאֹ יְרֹעָע [Is 16:10](https://ref.ly/Isa16:10) *a shout shall not be uttered* (of joy in harvest; ‖יְרֻנָּן). **Hithpō‘l.** *Impf.* 1 S. הִתְרוֺעָע [Psalm 108:10](https://ref.ly/Ps108:10); 3 mpl. יִתְרוֺעֲעוּ [65:14](https://ref.ly/Ps65:14); *Imv.* fs. הִתְרוֺעָעִי [60:10](https://ref.ly/Ps60:10);—**1\.** *shout in triumph:* c. על [Psalm 60:10](https://ref.ly/Ps60:10) (subj. Phil.; iron.) ‖ [108:10](https://ref.ly/Ps108:10) (subj. ´י:). **2\.** *shout for joy,* [Psalm 65:14](https://ref.ly/Ps65:14) (meadows, valleys; ‖שׁיר).

> \[Str7452,7453–54] † I. רֵעַ **n.verb.** shouting, roar, but dub.;—קוֺל הָעָם בְּרֵעֹה֯ [Ex 32:17](https://ref.ly/Exod32:17) (E) *sound of the people as they shouted* (read Inf. cstr. בָּרִעֹה for בְּהָרִיעוֺ, cf. √**5**); יַגִּיד עָלָיו רֵעָוֺ [Jb 36:33](https://ref.ly/Job36:33) *the roar thereof telleth of him* (\< רַעְמוֺ *his thunder,* so Bu); לָמָּה תָרִיעִי רֵעַ [Mi 4:9](https://ref.ly/Mic4:9) *why criest thou out with a crying?* i.e. so loudly (but v. √ **6\.** ).—II. III. רֵעַ v. רעהf

> \[Str8643] †תְּרוּעָה **n.f.** shout or blast of war, alarm, or joy;—´ת: [Lv 23:24](https://ref.ly/Lev23:24) \+; cstr. תְּרוּעַת [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19) \+;—**1\.** *alarm* of war, *war\-cry,* [Jos 6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20), [Je 20:16](https://ref.ly/Jer20:16), Ez [21:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:27), [Am 1:14](https://ref.ly/Amos1:14), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Amos2:2), [Zp 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:16), [Jb 39:25](https://ref.ly/Job39:25); מִלְחָמָה ´ת: [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19), [49:2](https://ref.ly/Jer49:2); מֶלָח ´ת: *battle\-cry of king* [Nu 23:21](https://ref.ly/Num23:21) (poem in JE). **2\.** *blast* for march: ´תקע ת: [Nu 10:5](https://ref.ly/Num10:5), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Num10:6), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Num10:6) (P; with חֲצֹצְרוֺת; hence) ´חֲצֹצְרוֺת הַתּ: [Nu 31:6](https://ref.ly/Num31:6) (P) [2 Ch 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:12); on day of atonement ´שׁוֺפַר ת: [Lv 25:9](https://ref.ly/Lev25:9) (H); 1st of mo. ´זִכְרוֺן ת: [23:24](https://ref.ly/Lev23:24) (P; בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִי כְּאֶחָד לַחֹדֶשׁ), יוֺם תֹ [Nu 29:1](https://ref.ly/Num29:1) (P); gen. ´צִלְצְלֵי ת: [Psalm 150:5](https://ref.ly/Ps150:5). **3\.** *shout of joy* with religious impulse, [1 S 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:5), [4:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:6), [4:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:6), [2 S 6:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:15) \= [1 Ch 15:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:28), [2 Ch 15:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:14), [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13); הַשִּמְחָה ´קוֺל ת: [Ezr 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13); in public worship gen. [Jb 33:26](https://ref.ly/Job33:26); esp. \+ musical service [Psalm 33:3](https://ref.ly/Ps33:3), [47:6](https://ref.ly/Ps47:6); ´זִבְחֵי ת: [27:6](https://ref.ly/Ps27:6), ´יֹדְעֵי ת: [89:16](https://ref.ly/Ps89:16). **4\.** *shout of joy,* in gen. [Jb 8:21](https://ref.ly/Job8:21) (‖שְחַוֺק).

## רוּץ (id: BDB08988)

\[Str7323,7442] † רוּץ **vb.** run (NH *id.* (rare); Eth. ሮፀ፡ Zinj. *Pf.* 1 S. רצת; \= Aram. רְהַט; ܪܗܰܛ (WSG 47\); cf. As. *râṣu, be helpful* (i.e. *run* to help?));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. רָץ [Gn 18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7); 2 ms. רַצְתָּה [Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5), etc.; *Impf.* יָרוּץ Hb [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hab2:2) \+, יָרֻץ [Jb 16:14](https://ref.ly/Job16:14), וַיָּ֫רָץ [Gn 18:2](https://ref.ly/Gen18:2) \+, וַיָּרָץ [2 S 18:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:21).; 1 S. sf. ארוצם [Je 50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44) Kt (but \< Qr. Hiph., v. infr.), etc. \[v. also רצץ]; *Imv.* ms. רוּץ [2 S 18:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:23) [2 K 4:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:26), רֻץ [1 S 20:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:36), [Zc 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zech2:8); *Inf. cstr.* לָרוּץ [1 S 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:6) \+; *Pt.* רָץ [2 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:22) \+, pl. רָצִים [1 S 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:17) \+, רָצִין [2 K 11:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:13) (Ges§ 87 e);—**1\.** *run,* c. אֶל loc. [Gn 18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7), [24:20](https://ref.ly/Gen24:20) (both J), [Nu 17:12](https://ref.ly/Num17:12) (P), אֶל Pers. [Gn 24:29](https://ref.ly/Gen24:29) (J; \+ ה loc.), [1 S 3:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:5), [Is 55:5](https://ref.ly/Isa55:5) (fig.), with hostility, *against,* [Jb 15:26](https://ref.ly/Job15:26) (fig.), so goat against ram [Dn 8:6](https://ref.ly/Dan8:6), עַל Pers., *against* [Jb 16:14](https://ref.ly/Job16:14); ה loc. also [Jos 7:22](https://ref.ly/Josh7:22), לְבֵיתוֺ [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9), לָרַע [Is 59:7](https://ref.ly/Isa59:7), [Pr 1:16](https://ref.ly/Prov1:16); acc. loc. [1 S 4:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:12), so MT [2 K 23:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:12) (\= *he went quickly thence,* but text perh. corrupt, v. Kit Benz, and cf. **Hiph.** infr.); אַחֲרֵי Pers. [2 S 15:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:1), [1 K 1:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:5) (both of royal escort, *out\-runners*), [18:46](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:46) (\+ עַד loc.), לִפְנֵי rei [1 S 8:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:11); אֵת Pers. accomp. [Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5); c. acc. of way, אֹרַֹח [Psalm 19:6](https://ref.ly/Ps19:6), דֶּרֶח [2 S 18:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:23), [Psalm 119:132](https://ref.ly/Ps119:132) (fig.); as Prelim., \+ inf. לִקְרַאת *run to meet* [Gn 18:2](https://ref.ly/Gen18:2), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17), [29:13](https://ref.ly/Gen29:13), [33:4](https://ref.ly/Gen33:4) (all J), [2 K 4:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:26), [Je 51:31](https://ref.ly/Jer51:31), \+ vb. fin. [Jb 9:25](https://ref.ly/Job9:25) (as swift); הָרָצִים [2 Ch 30:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:6), [30:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:10), [Est 3:13](https://ref.ly/Esth3:13), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Esth3:15), riders בַּסּוּסִים ´הָר: [8:10](https://ref.ly/Esth8:10), רֹבֵי הָרֶכֶשׁ ´הָר: v:[14](https://ref.ly/Esth8:14). **Pō‘lēl** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יְרוֺצֵָצוּ [Na 2:5](https://ref.ly/Nah2:5) *they run swiftly, dart* (like lightning; of chariots). **Hiph.** *cause to run:* **1\.** *bring,* or *move, quickly, Impf.* 3 mpl. sf. וַיְרִיצֻהוּ, מִן loc. [Gn 41:14](https://ref.ly/Gen41:14) (E), rd. perh. also 3 ms. וַיָּרֶץ [2 K 23:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:12) (acc. om.; for MT וַיָּרָץ v. **Qal**); ל Pers., acc. rei om. וַיָּרִיצוּ [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13); *Imv.* ms. הָרֵץ [1 S 17:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:17) (\+ acc. loc., ל Pers.); *Impf.* 3 fs. תָּרִיץ יָדָיו [Psalm 68:32](https://ref.ly/Ps68:32) *quickly stretch out* hands, לֵאלֹהִים (viz. with offerings). **2\.** c. מֵעַל \= *drive away from,* 1 S., sf. 3 ms., אֲרִיצֶנּוּ [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19), so read prob. also in ‖ [50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44) (Qr אֲרִיצֵם, \> Kt ארוצם).

## רָזָה (id: BDB08995)

\[Str7329] †רָזָה **vb.** be or grow lean (Ar. رَدِيَ *grow thin and weak*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. רָזָה [Zp 2:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:11) obj. false gods, appar. *make lean* (Vulgate *attenuavit;* LXX is ἐξολεθρύσει), but sense strange, \< רִזָּה (v. Ar. supr.), yet even this very doubtful. **Niph.** *be made lean, Impf.* 3 ms. מִשְׁמַן בְּשָרוֺ יֵרָזֶה [Is 17:4](https://ref.ly/Isa17:4).

> \[Str7330] † \[רָזֶה] **adj.** lean (on formation cf. LagBN 49\);—fs. רָזָה, Ez [24:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:20) (of שֶה; opp. בִּרְיָה), of land, \= barren, [Nu 13:20](https://ref.ly/Num13:20) (JE; opp. שְׁמֵנָה).

> \[Str7334] † רָזִי **n.**\[**m.**] leanness, wasting;—רָזִי־לִי [Is 24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Isa24:16) *to me, wasting!* (opp. צְבִי).

> \[Str7332,7333] † I. רָזוֺן **n.**\[**m.**] leanness, wasting, scantness;—abs. ´ר:: **1\.** *leanness* [Is 10:16](https://ref.ly/Isa10:16) (fig.; opp. מִשְׁמַנָּיו). **2\.** *wasting,* i.e. wasting disease, [Psalm 106:15](https://ref.ly/Ps106:15) (cf. Bae). **3\.** ´אֵיפַת ר: [Mi 6:10](https://ref.ly/Mic6:10) *ephah of scantness,* scant measure.—II. רָזוֺן v. רזן. below

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 24:20; Isaiah 10:16; Micah 6:10; Psalms 106:15; Isaiah 24:16; Isaiah 17:4; Numbers 13:20; Zephaniah 2:11
* **Associated Articles:** רָזַן (ID: `BDB09003@BDBHebrewLexicon`)

3 fs. וְרָֽחֲבָה וְנָֽסְבָה Ez [41:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:7) prob. err., Co רָחְבָּהּ וְנוֺסָפָה, Toy וְהָרֹחַב נוֺסַף, Krae וְיָֽסְפוּ רָֽחֲבוּ \[וְ?]. **Niph.** *Pt.* as adj. נִרְחָב [Is 30:23](https://ref.ly/Isa30:23) *a broad, roomy, pasture.* **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִרְחִיב [Gn 26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22) Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5), הִרְחִָב [Is 30:33](https://ref.ly/Isa30:33); \+ הֶעְמִיק); *make wide,* acc. couch [Is 57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8) (fig.). **2\.** *enlarge* limit of territory (גְּבוּל) [Am 1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13), [Ex 34:24](https://ref.ly/Exod34:24) (J), [Dt 12:20](https://ref.ly/Deut12:20), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Deut19:8), so ָ\+גּד ´מַר: [33:20](https://ref.ly/Deut33:20), cf. מְקוֺם אָהֳלֵח ´הַר: [Is 54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2) (fig.); ## I רחם (id: BDB09024)

I. רחם (√ of foll.; orig. meaning dub.; Thes *be soft,* so Gerber126, cp. Ar. رَخُمَ *be soft, gentle* (but خ orig. cons.?); DlHWB 604 b *be wide;* cf. As. *rîmu, rêmu,* Ar. رَحِمٌ, NH רֶחֶם, Aram. רַחֲמָא, ܪܰܚܡܳܐ, all *womb;* MI17 רחמת *female captives;* vbs. (connexion with רֶחֶם not wholly clear, cf. NöZMG xi (1886\), 151 f., v. רַחֲמִים): As. *râmu, love, compassionate,* Ar. رَحِمَ *have compassion,* also رَخِمَ *be inclined toward, affectionate to* ( \= رحم acc. to Ar. authorities, v. Lane); Sab. epith. dei רחמנן *Compassionate* ( \= Ar. الرَّحْمَانُ), CISiv. no. 6\. 3 FellZMG liv (1900\), 252 who cp. epith. רחַם *id.;* cf. NH רָחַם Pi.; Thes cp. NöZMG xi (1886\), 727\));—**Qal**:69 *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ר: [Is 4:4](https://ref.ly/Isa4:4) \+, etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְחַץ [Dt 23:12](https://ref.ly/Deut23:12) \+ etc.; *Imv.* ms. רְחַץ [2 S 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:8), [2 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:13); mpl. רַחֲצוּ [Gn 18:4](https://ref.ly/Gen18:4) \+; *Inf. cstr.* רְחֹץ [Gn 24:32](https://ref.ly/Gen24:32) \+, לְרָחְצָה [Ex 30:18](https://ref.ly/Exod30:18) \+; *Pt.* fs. רֹחֶ֫צֶת [2 S 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:2), fpl. רֹחֲצוֺת Ct [5:12](https://ref.ly/Song5:12);—**1\.** trans. *wash* (with water), c. acc. feet (רגלים; בַּמַּיִם oft. expr.), [Gn 18:4](https://ref.ly/Gen18:4), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), [24:32](https://ref.ly/Gen24:32), [43:24](https://ref.ly/Gen43:24) (all J), Ju [19:21](https://ref.ly/Judg19:21), [1 S 25:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:41), [2 S 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:8), Ct [5:3](https://ref.ly/Song5:3); פְּעָמָיו בְּדַם הָרָשָׁע ´יִר: [Psalm 58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11) (fig. of righteous, at ´י:’s vengeance); c. acc. hands (יָדַיִם) [Dt 21:6](https://ref.ly/Deut21:6) (\+ עַל *over,* in ceremonial, to express innocence), hence fig. כַּפַּי בְּנַקָּיוֺן ´אֶר: [Psalm 26:6](https://ref.ly/Ps26:6), [73:13](https://ref.ly/Ps73:13); hands and feet [Ex 30:19](https://ref.ly/Exod30:19) (\+ מִן of laver), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod30:21), [40:31](https://ref.ly/Exod40:31) (\+ מִן *id.;* all P); c. acc. face (פָּנָיו) [Gn 43:31](https://ref.ly/Gen43:31) (J); c. acc. Pers. Ez [16:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:9), [Ex 29:4](https://ref.ly/Exod29:4), [40:12](https://ref.ly/Exod40:12), [Lv 8:6](https://ref.ly/Lev8:6) (all P), acc. בְּשָרוֺ (oft. ‖כבּס of garments), [Lv 14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Lev15:13) \+ 9 t. ## רָחַשׁ (id: BDB09050)

\[Str7370] † רָחַשׁ **vb.** keep moving, stir (NH *stir, move,* esp. of lips in speech, also, in expl. of מרחשׁת, of *stewing, boiling;* Aram. רְחֵישׁ, ܪܚܶܫ, ܪܚܰܫ, *creep, move, flutter,* ܪܰܚܫܳܐ *reptile*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. לִבִּי דָּבָר טוֺב ´ר: [Psalm 45:2](https://ref.ly/Ps45:2) *is astir.*

> \[Str7371] † רַ֫חַת **n.\[f.]** winnowing\-shovel, or the like (√ unknown; Thes fr. רוח \= *ventilabrum,* cf. NöZMG xl (1886\), 728; Wetzst in DeIs (2\), 709 fr. רחה \= رَخُوَ *be soft, loose, friable,* etc., cf. Köii. 1, 177; NH \= BH, Ar. رَخْتٌ is prob. loan\-wd. Nöl.c.);—וּבַמִּזְרֶה ´זֹרֶה בָר: [Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:2; Leviticus 2:7; Isaiah 30:24; Leviticus 7:9

\[Str7372] † \[רָטֵב, רָטֹב LagBN 31] **vb.** be moist (NH *id.;* As. *raṭâbu,* II. *moisten;* Ar. رَطِبَ, Eth. ረጥበ፡ Aram. רְטַב, ܪܛܶܒ, all *be moist*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. מִזֶּרֶם הָרִים יִרְטָבוּ [Jb 24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 24:8; Job 8:16
