## רָטַשׁ (id: BDB09059)

\[Str7376] † \[רָטַשׁ] **vb. Pi.** dash in pieces (cf. Targum רְטַשׁ cast away, reject; but possibly akin to לָטַשׁ);—**Pi.** *Impf.* 2 ms. תְּרַטֵּשׁ [2 K 8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12) their children *thou will dash in pieces;* 3 fpl. קְשָׁתוֺת נְעָרִיםשׁ תְּרַטַּשְׁנָה [Is 13:18](https://ref.ly/Isa13:18), but prob. crpt., Du conj. נְעָרוֺת תְּרֻטַּשְׁנָה cf. Che. **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 fs. אֵם עַ־ל בָּנִים רֻטָּשָׁה [Ho 14:14](https://ref.ly/Hos14:14); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יְרֻטְּשׁוּ [Is 13:16](https://ref.ly/Isa13:16), [Na 3:10](https://ref.ly/Nah3:10), יְרֻטָּשׁוּ [Ho 14:1](https://ref.ly/Hos14:1), all of children.

> \[Str7377] רִי v. רוה

## ריף (id: BDB09075)

ריף (√ of following; meaning unknown).

> \[Str7383] † \[רִיפָה] **n.\[f.]** dub.; some grain or fruit (such as was spread out to dry (2 S), and also pounded (Pr); Vulgate2 S *as if drying barley groats*, LXXL παλάθας *cakes of preserved fruit*, cf. preparation of ‘apricot\-cheese’ WetzstZPV xiv. 2 NesMarg.18, who cites also AlmkvistKl. Beltr. zur Lexlcogr. des Vulg. Ar. Actes. 419\);—pl. abs. רִפוֺת [2 S 17:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:19), רִיפוֺת [Pr 27:22](https://ref.ly/Prov27:22).

> \[Str7384] † רִיפַת **n.pr.gent.** ‘son’ of Gomer (of Japhet);—[Gn 10:2](https://ref.ly/Gen10:2) (P; \> ‖ [1 Ch 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:6) דִּיפַת q.v.); Joseph. makes \= Paphlagonians; Bo LagGes. ## רִיק (id: BDB09078)

\[Str7324] † \[רִיק] **vb. Hiph.** make empty, empty out (Ar. رَاقَ (ي) *pour out, forth*, intrans. (of water, blood, etc.); Aram. רִיק *empty, pour*, ܪܦ in deriv.; As *rêḳu, empty*);—*Pf.* 1 S. הֲרִי֫קֹתִי [Lv 26:33](https://ref.ly/Lev26:33), [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10); 3 pl. חֵרִיקוּ Ez [28:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:7), [30:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:11); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָרִיק Hb [1:17](https://ref.ly/Hab1:17), וַיָּ֫רֶק [Gn 14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14) (but v. infr.); *Imv.* ms. הָרֵק [Psalm 35:8](https://ref.ly/Ps35:8); *Inf. cstr.* הָרִיק [Is 32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6); *Pt.* pl. מְרִיקִים [Gn 42:35](https://ref.ly/Gen42:35), [Zc 4:12](https://ref.ly/Zech4:12);—**1\.** *empty* vessels [Gn 42:35](https://ref.ly/Gen42:35) (E) [Je 48:12](https://ref.ly/Jer48:12); *keep empty*, לְחֶרִיק נֶפֶשׁ רָעֵב [Is 32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6) (i.e. keep hungry). **2\.** *pour out* or *down*, rain [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10), [Ec 11:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:3), oil [Zc 4:12](https://ref.ly/Zech4:12). **3\.** *empty out* (i.e. *draw*) sword (cf. As. *kakkê ittabbaku* \[pass.], DlHWB 699 b) [Ex 15:9](https://ref.ly/Exod15:9) (poem), [Lv 26:33](https://ref.ly/Lev26:33) (H), Ez [5:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:2), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:12), [12:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:14), [28:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:7), [30:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:11); prob. also Hb [1:17](https://ref.ly/Hab1:17) (חרבו for חרמוֺ Gie We Now); of lance [Psalm 35:3](https://ref.ly/Ps35:3); cf. (several.) וירק חניכיו [Gn 14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14) (R) *led forth*, \< Sam. וַיָּ֫דֶק (Aram. דּוּק Aph. *look*) i.e. *mustered* LXX cf. Di al., אֲדִקֵּד Ball.— In [Psalm 18:43](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43) LXX SyrVer Hup\-Now Bae rd. אֲדִקֵּם; so ‖ [2 S 22:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:43) MT; but De Dr \< אֲרִיקֵם (v. דקק). **Hoph.** *be emptied out* from vessel : *Pf.* 3 ms. הוּרק [Je 48:11](https://ref.ly/Jer48:11); *Impf.* 2 ms. תּוּרַק Ct [1:3](https://ref.ly/Song1:3) (al. 3 fs.; of ointment, in sim.); cf. also [Am 6:6](https://ref.ly/Amos6:6), where Oort מוּרְקֵּי for מִזְרְקֵי.

> \[Str7386] † \[רֵיק], רֵק **adj.** empty, vain;— m. abs. רֵק [Gn 37:24](https://ref.ly/Gen37:24) \+ 2 t.; f. דֵקָה f. דֵקָה Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11), [Is 29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8); mpl. קִים(י)רֵ Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16) \+ ; fpl. רֵקוֺת [Gn 41:27](https://ref.ly/Gen41:27);—**1\.** *empty*, of vessels [2 K 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:3), Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16), Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11) \+ (prob.) [Je 14:3](https://ref.ly/Jer14:3) (רֵיקִים for דֵיקָם), [51:34](https://ref.ly/Jer51:34) (רֵיק for רִיק); of pit [Gn 37:24](https://ref.ly/Gen37:24) (J), lap [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13), ears of grain [Gn 41:27](https://ref.ly/Gen41:27) (E; דֵּקּוֺת v:[6](https://ref.ly/Gen41:6), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen41:7), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen41:23), v:[24](https://ref.ly/Gen41:24)); רֵקָה נַפְשׁוֺ [Is 29:8](https://ref.ly/Isa29:8) (cf. [32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6) √ **1\.**). **2\.** *empty, idle, worthless*, ethically; אנשׁים ריקים *worthless fellows* Ju [9:4](https://ref.ly/Judg9:4), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Judg11:3), [2 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:7), so הָרֵקִים alone (as subst.) [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20); מְרַדֵּף רֵיקִים [Pr 12:11](https://ref.ly/Prov12:11) AVRV of persons; \< *vain, unprofitable things*, [28:19](https://ref.ly/Prov28:19) (v. Toy); מִן ´דָּבָר ר: [Dt 32:47](https://ref.ly/Deut32:47) (D) *a thing too empty* (of significance) for you; \+ (prob.) [Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3) (רֵיק for MT רִיק) *love an empty thing* (‖כזב), of abortive course of action.

## רָכַךְ (id: BDB09096)

\[Str7401] † \[רָכַךְ] **vb.** be tender, weak, soft (Ar. رَكَّ Aram. רְכַח (in der. Spec. and deriv.), ܪܰܟ, *id.*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. רַח [2 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:19), [2 Ch 34:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:27); 3 pl. רַכּוּ [Psalm 55:22](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22); *Impf.* יֵרַח (Ges§ 67 p) [Is 7:4](https://ref.ly/Isa7:4) \+ ;—**1\.** *be tender, weak*, of heart : **a.** *be timid, fearful*, ‖יַרֵא, [Is 7:4](https://ref.ly/Isa7:4), [Je 51:46](https://ref.ly/Jer51:46), [Dt 20:3](https://ref.ly/Deut20:3). **b.** be *softened, penitent*, ‖כָּנַע Niph., [2 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:19) \= [2 Ch 34:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:27). **2\.** be soft, of treacherous words [Psalm 55:22](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22) (c. מִן comp.; ‖ II. חָלַק). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 fs. רֻכְּבָה [Is 1:6](https://ref.ly/Isa1:6) *it has* not *been softened*, mollified, with oil (of wound, מַכָּךְ). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְאֵל הֵרַח לִבִּי [Jb 23:16](https://ref.ly/Job23:16) (Ges§ 67 v), causat. Of **Qal 1 a**.

> \[Str7390] † רַךְ **adj.** tender, delicate, soft;—´ר: abs. [Gn 18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7) \+, cstr. [Dt 20:8](https://ref.ly/Deut20:8), [2 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:7); fs. רֵכָּה [Dt 28:56](https://ref.ly/Deut28:56) \+ ; mpl. רֵבִּים [Gn 33:13](https://ref.ly/Gen33:13); fpl. abs. רַכּוֺת [29:17](https://ref.ly/Gen29:17), [Jb 40:27](https://ref.ly/Job40:27);—**1\.** *tender* of flesh [Gn 18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7) (J; בֶּן־בֶּקָר); *tender, delicate*, esp. in body, of children [33:13](https://ref.ly/Gen33:13) (J), cf. (implying weakness of undeveloped character) [2 S 3:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:39), נַעַר וָרָח [1 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:5), [29:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:1), (וְיָתִיד) רֵךְ [Pr 4:3](https://ref.ly/Prov4:3) (‘of tenderage,’ Toy, q.v.); of man ( \+ עָנֹג; i.e. delicately nurtured) [Dt 28:54](https://ref.ly/Deut28:54), so of woman v:[56](https://ref.ly/Deut28:56) ( \+ עֲנֻגָּה), so, fig. of Bab., [Is 47:1](https://ref.ly/Isa47:1) ( \+ *id.*; רַךְ as subst. \=*tender* (twing) Ez [17:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:22) (in fig.); *delicate, weak*, of eyes [Gn 29:17](https://ref.ly/Gen29:17) (E). **2\.** לֵבָב(ה)נח *weak of heart*, timid, [Dt 20:8](https://ref.ly/Deut20:8) ( \+ יָרֵא), [2 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:7) (נַעַד). **3\.** *soft*, of words, abs. רַכּוֺת [Jb 40:27](https://ref.ly/Job40:27) \= subst., mild, gentle words (‖תַּחֲנוּנִים); מַעֲנֶה רַךְ [Pr 15:1](https://ref.ly/Prov15:1) (opp. דְּבַר עֶצֶב), לָשׁוֺן רַכָּה [25:15](https://ref.ly/Prov25:15).

## רָכַל (id: BDB09100)

\[Str7402] † \[רָכַל] **vb.** prob. go about, from one to another (for trade or gossip) (? connected with רגל; cf. Ar. رَكَلَ *kick* a horse, to make him go \[? prop. *use the foot*, cf. رجل ?]; NH רוֺכֵל, Aram. רוֺכְלָא, ܪܰܟܳܠܴܐ *trader*, NH רְכִילוּת *slander* \[cf. مَاشٍ *calumniator*, fr. مَشَى *go about*, Qor 2:19 al.]);—**Qal** *Pt.* רוֺכֵל Ct [3:6](https://ref.ly/Song3:6); fs. cstr. רֹכֶ֫לֶת Ez [27:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:3), sf. רֹכַלְתֵח v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:20), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:23); mpl. רֹכְלִים [1 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:15) \+ , etc.;—as subst. \= *trafficker, trader:* usu. pl., [Na 3:16](https://ref.ly/Nah3:16), Ez [17:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:4) (´עִיר ר:), [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:13), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:15), [27:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:17), [27:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:22), [27:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:22), [27:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:23) a (\< del. Toy Krae), v:[24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24), [Ne 3:31](https://ref.ly/Neh3:31), [3:32](https://ref.ly/Neh3:32), [13:20](https://ref.ly/Neh13:20) ( \+ מֹרֵי כָל־מִמְכָּר); ´מִסְחַר הָר: [1 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:15) (text dub.; cf. ´מ: p.695\); sg. fig. of Tyre, רֹכֶלֶת הָעַמִּים אֶל־אִיִּים רַכִּים Ez [27:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:8), of a people v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:20), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:23) b (pl. MezḤarran 34 Krae, v. also כִּלְמֶד); sg. of Pers. only אַבְקַת רוֺכֵל Ct [3:6](https://ref.ly/Song3:6).

## רָכַס (id: BDB09105)

\[Str7405] † \[רָכַס] **vb.** bind (As. *rakâsu, id.;* NH *found, lay foundation* (rare); Ar. رَكَسَ *bind with* رِكَاسٌ \[rope tying camel’s head to forefoot] (Frey); but usu. *turn over, reverse* (Lane));—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִרְכְּסוּ (P) [Ex 28:28](https://ref.ly/Exod28:28), וַיִּרְכְּסוּ [39:21](https://ref.ly/Exod39:21), *bind* חשֶׁן by (מִן) its rings to (אֶל) rings of ephod.

> \[Str7406] † \[רֶ֫כֶס] **n.\[m.]** dub.; perh. *roughness* (of ground ; ? lit. *binding, knot*); or bound up, impeded, i.e. *the impassable*; or mountain\-*chain* (v. Thes);—pl. הָֽרְכָסִים [Is 40:4](https://ref.ly/Isa40:4) (‖הֶעָקֹכ; opp. בִּקְעָה).

> \[Str7407] † \[רֹ֫כֶס] **n.\[m.]** dub., Thes Hup\-Now *snare*, or *band* (Dr *banding together*), or (most) *league, conspiracy* (RV *plottings*); Ol reads deriv. of רכל \= *slander*, so (רְכִילֵי) Che Du; Bae despairs;—pl. cstr. דֻכְסֵי־אִישׁ [Psalm 31:21](https://ref.ly/Ps31:21) (‖דִיב לְשֹׁנוֺת).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 40:4; Psalms 31:21; Exodus 28:28; Exodus 39:21

## רָמַש (id: BDB09145)

\[Str7430] †רָמַש **vb.** creep, move lightly, move about (chiefly P) (Ar. رَمَشَ *touch gently* (Kam Frey); NH רְמָשִים *creeping things* (rare));—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּרָמשֹׁ [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* רֹמֵש [1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26) \+; fs. רֹמֶ֫שֶת [Lv 11:46](https://ref.ly/Lev11:46) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** subj. ground, הָאֲדָמָה ´כֹּל אֲשֶׁר ת: *all with which the ground creeps* (teems), i.e. all creeping things, [Lv 20:55](https://ref.ly/Lev20:55) (H), [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) (P). **2\.** elsewhere subj. animal: **a.** *creep,* עַל־הָאֲדָמָה [Dt 4:18](https://ref.ly/Deut4:18), [Gn 7:8](https://ref.ly/Gen7:8) (P), עַל־הָאָרֶץ [Lv 11:44](https://ref.ly/Lev11:44) (H), [Gn 1:30](https://ref.ly/Gen1:30) (P); הָרֶמֶש הָרֹמֵש עַל־הָאֲדָמָה Ez [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:20), עַל־הָאָרֶץ [Gn 1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:17) (all P). **b.** *move lightly, glide about,* of water animals, כָּל־נֶפֶש (בַּמַּיִם) הַחַיָּה הָרֹמֶצֶת [Lv 11:46](https://ref.ly/Lev11:46) (H), [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21) (P), cf. [Psalm 69:35](https://ref.ly/Ps69:35). **c.** in gen. *move about,* of all land\-animals, עַל־הָאָרֶץ [Gn 1:28](https://ref.ly/Gen1:28), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Gen7:21), [8:19](https://ref.ly/Gen8:19) (all P); specif. of wild beasts prowling at night [Psalm 104:20](https://ref.ly/Ps104:20).

> \[Str7431] †רֶ֫מֶש **n.m.**:Ez [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:20) **coll.** creeping things, moving things;—´ר: abs. [1 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13) \+, cstr. [Ho 2:20](https://ref.ly/Hos2:20) \+;—**1\.** *creeping things* (disting. from בְּהֵמָה, חַיָּה, עוֺף, צִפּוֺר, דָּג) [Ho 2:20](https://ref.ly/Hos2:20), [1 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13), Ez [8:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:10), [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:20), [Gn 1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Gen1:25), [1:26](https://ref.ly/Gen1:26), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Gen6:20), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:17), [8:19](https://ref.ly/Gen8:19) (all P), Hb [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hab1:14) (in sim) [Psalm 148:10](https://ref.ly/Ps148:10). **2\.** of sea animals, *gliding things,* [Psalm 104:25](https://ref.ly/Ps104:25). **3\.** *moving things,* of all animals [Gn 9:3](https://ref.ly/Gen9:3) (P).—Cf. ## רָנַן (id: BDB09150)

\[Str7442,7323,7444] †רָנַן] **vb.** give a ringing cry (onomatop.? cf. Ar. رَنَّ *cry aloud,* also *twang* (of bowstring); NH Pi. *murmur, complain,* cf. JAram. (not Targum) **)**;—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יָרוּן (Ges§ 67 q) [Pr 29:6](https://ref.ly/Prov29:6) (but rd. perhaps יָרוּץ, Pinsk Toy, v. רוּץ **2**); 3 fs. תָּרֹן [Is 35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6); 3 mpl. יָרֹ֫נוּ [24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14) \+, ´וַיָּר: [Lv 9:24](https://ref.ly/Lev9:24); 3 fpl. תָּרֹ֫נָּה [Pr 1:20](https://ref.ly/Prov1:20), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Prov8:3); *Imv. fs.* רָנִּי [Is 54:1](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1) \+, רֹ֫נִּי [12:6](https://ref.ly/Isa12:6); mpl. רָנּוּ [Je 31:7](https://ref.ly/Jer31:7) \+; *Inf. cstr.* בְּרָ־ן [Jb 38:7](https://ref.ly/Job38:7);—*give a ringing cry:* **1\.** in joy, exultation, ‖צָהַל [Je 31:7](https://ref.ly/Jer31:7) (שִמְחָה ´ר:), [Is 12:6](https://ref.ly/Isa12:6), [24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14) (‖נָשָא קוֺל) [54:1](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1) (\+רִנָּהפָּחַךְ); ‖הֵרִיעַ [Zp 3:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:14) (\+שמח, עלז), [Is 44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23), [Jb 38:7](https://ref.ly/Job38:7); ‖שָמַח [Zc 2:14](https://ref.ly/Zech2:14), [Psalm 35:27](https://ref.ly/Ps35:27), [Pr 29:6](https://ref.ly/Prov29:6) (v. supr.); ‖גִּיל [Is 49:13](https://ref.ly/Isa49:13) (\+הֵרִים רִנָּה), ‖צָוַח [42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11); opp. צָעַק (*cry* in distress) [65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14) (מִן of cause); [Lv 9:24](https://ref.ly/Lev9:24), [Is 35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6) (subj. לָשׁוֺן).—[Is 61:7](https://ref.ly/Isa61:7) is corrupt; read perh. וָרֹק *and spitting,* Klo CheHpt. **2\.** in distress, [La 2:19](https://ref.ly/Lam2:19). **3\.** *cry aloud,* in summons, exhortation (of wisdom) [Pr 1:20](https://ref.ly/Prov1:20) (‖נָתַן קוֺל), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Prov8:3). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 pl. consec. וְרִנְּנוּ [Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12), [51:48](https://ref.ly/Jer51:48); *Impf.* 3 fs. חְ֔רַנֵּן [Psalm 51:16](https://ref.ly/Ps51:16), 3 mpl. יְרַנְּנוּ [84:3](https://ref.ly/Ps84:3) \+, יְרֵַ\+נּנוּ [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ps5:12) \+; 3 fpl. חְ֔רַנֵּ֫נָּה [71:23](https://ref.ly/Ps71:23) (van d. H. תְּרַנֵּנָה), etc.; *Imv.* mpl. רַנְּנוּ [Is 52:9](https://ref.ly/Isa52:9) \+; *Inf. abs.* רַנֵּן [Psalm 132:16](https://ref.ly/Ps132:16), *cstr. id.* [Is 35:2](https://ref.ly/Isa35:2);—*give a ringing cry,* in joy, exultation, esp. in praise to ´י:, [Je 31:12](https://ref.ly/Jer31:12), [51:48](https://ref.ly/Jer51:48), [Is 26:19](https://ref.ly/Isa26:19), [35:2](https://ref.ly/Isa35:2) (\+גִּילַת, v. גִּילָה), [52:8](https://ref.ly/Isa52:8) (‖נָשָא קוֺל) v:[9](https://ref.ly/Isa52:9) (\+פָּצַח); elsewh. only Psalm Psalm: [Psalm 5:12](https://ref.ly/Ps5:12) (‖שָמַח, עָלַץ), [67:5](https://ref.ly/Ps67:5), [90:14](https://ref.ly/Ps90:14), [92:5](https://ref.ly/Ps92:5) (c. ב rei; all ‖ ´ש: alone); [149:5](https://ref.ly/Ps149:5) (‖עָלַז); [98:4](https://ref.ly/Ps98:4) (‖הֵרִיעַ; \+פָּצַח, זִמֵּר), [132:9](https://ref.ly/Ps132:9), [145:7](https://ref.ly/Ps145:7), רַנֵּן יְרֵַ\+נּנוּ [132:16](https://ref.ly/Ps132:16); c. acc. of theme [51:16](https://ref.ly/Ps51:16), [59:17](https://ref.ly/Ps59:17) (‖שִׁיר); ´בי: [33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1); ב rei [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ps20:6), [63:8](https://ref.ly/Ps63:8), [89:13](https://ref.ly/Ps89:13); c. ´לי: [95:1](https://ref.ly/Ps95:1) (‖הֵרִיעַ), אֶל־אֵל [84:3](https://ref.ly/Ps84:3), ´לפני י:(מִ) [96:12](https://ref.ly/Ps96:12) (‖עָלַז) \= [1 Ch 16:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:33) (‖עָלַץ), [Psalm 98:8](https://ref.ly/Ps98:8); subj. שְכָּתַיִם [71:23](https://ref.ly/Ps71:23) (‖זִמֵּר). **Pu.** *Impf.* 3 ms. impers. לאֹ יְרֻנָּן [Is 16:10](https://ref.ly/Isa16:10) *no ringing cry shall be given* (‖יְרֹעַָע). **Hiph.** *Impf. cause to ring out* for joy, 1 s. לֵב אַלְמָנָה אַרְנִן [Jb 29:13](https://ref.ly/Job29:13); 2 ms. בֹקֶר וָעֶרֶב תַּרְנִין ´מוֺצָאֵ: [Psalm 65:9](https://ref.ly/Ps65:9); *Imv.* mpl. הַרְנִינוּ *ring out a cry* of joy [Psalm 32:11](https://ref.ly/Ps32:11) (‖שמח, גיל), ´לי: [81:2](https://ref.ly/Ps81:2) (‖הריע); c. acc. of theme, עַמּוֺ [Dt 32:43](https://ref.ly/Deut32:43) (v. Dr; cf. **Pi.**).—**Hithpōl.** [Psalm 78:65](https://ref.ly/Ps78:65) cf. רון. p. 929

> \[Str7438] †\[רֹן] **n.\[m.]** ringing cry; pl. cstr. רָנֵּי פַלֵּט [Psalm 32:7](https://ref.ly/Ps32:7).

> \[Str7445] †רְנָנָה **n.f.** id.;—´ר: abs., of joy [Jb 3:7](https://ref.ly/Job3:7) [Psalm 100:2](https://ref.ly/Ps100:2) (‖שִמְחָה); pl. שִפְתֵי רְנָנוֺת [63:6](https://ref.ly/Ps63:6); exultation, sg. cstr. רִנְנַת רְשָׁעִים [Jb 20:5](https://ref.ly/Job20:5) (‖שִמְחָה).

> \[Str7440] †רִנָּה **n.f.** ringing cry;—abs. ´ר: [Je 7:16](https://ref.ly/Jer7:16) \+; sf. רִנָּתִי [Psalm 17:1](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1) \+, רִנָּתָם [Je 14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12) \+;—*ringing cry:* **1\.** in entreaty, supplication, to ´י:, [1 K 8:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:28) \= [2 Ch 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:19), [Je 7:16](https://ref.ly/Jer7:16), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Jer11:14), [Psalm 17:1](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1), [61:2](https://ref.ly/Ps61:2), [88:3](https://ref.ly/Ps88:3) (all \+תְּפִלָּה), [106:44](https://ref.ly/Ps106:44), [119:169](https://ref.ly/Ps119:169), [142:7](https://ref.ly/Ps142:7), [Je 14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12). **2\.** in proclamation [1 K 22:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:36). **3\.** in joy, esp. praise to ´י:, \+שִמְחָה [Is 35:10](https://ref.ly/Isa35:10), [51:11](https://ref.ly/Isa51:11) (\+שָשוֺן); \+תְּהַלָּה [2 Ch 20:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:22), \+שָשוֺן [Psalm 105:43](https://ref.ly/Ps105:43), \+תּוֺדָה [107:22](https://ref.ly/Ps107:22), \+עלץ [Pr 11:10](https://ref.ly/Prov11:10); ‖שְחוֺק [Psalm 126:2](https://ref.ly/Ps126:2); opp. בְּכִי, etc. [30:6](https://ref.ly/Ps30:6), [126:6](https://ref.ly/Ps126:6), cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps126:5); ´קוֺל ר: [Is 48:20](https://ref.ly/Isa48:20), [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5) (\+תּוֺדָה), [47:2](https://ref.ly/Ps47:2), [118:15](https://ref.ly/Ps118:15) (\+יְשׁוּעָה); פָּצַח ´ר: [Is 14:7](https://ref.ly/Isa14:7), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23), [49:13](https://ref.ly/Isa49:13), [54:1](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1), [55:12](https://ref.ly/Isa55:12); of ´י:’s joy over Zion [Zp 3:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:17);—בָּאֳנִיּוֺת רִנָּתָם [Is 43:14](https://ref.ly/Isa43:14) *in the ships of their ringing cry,* in which they exulted, but dub.; Hi Ew בַּאֲנִיּוֺת (bring down) *into mourning their* ´ר:; cf. Kit\-Di Du CheHpt Marti.

## רסן (id: BDB09157)

רסן (√of following; cf. Ar. رَسَنٌ (Frä100 f.) \= רֶסֶן; Targum רִסְנָא (rare), cf. DalmWB).

> \[Str7448] †I. רֶ֫סֶן **n.m.**:[Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28): **1\.** halter; **2\.** jaw (?);—**1\.** abs. ´ר: [Psalm 32:9](https://ref.ly/Ps32:9) restraining mouth of horse or ass (\+מֶתֶג); fig. of ´שֵׁם י:, עַל־לְחָיֵי עַמִּים´ר: [Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28); מִפָּנַי שִׁלֵָּחוּ ´ר: [Jb 30:11](https://ref.ly/Job30:11), i.e. threw off restraint. **2\.** sf. כֶּפֶל רִסְנוֺ [41:5](https://ref.ly/Job41:5) *the double of his jaw,* his double jaws (of croc.), si vera 1\.; connexion of mngs. strange, ‖פְּנֵי לְבוּשׁוֺ, whence GHBW right Du conj. סִיֹנוֺ.

> \[Str7449] † II. רֶ֫סֶן **n.pr.loc.** in Assyria, near Nineveh [Gn 10:12](https://ref.ly/Gen10:12); Δασεμ (prob. \= *rêšêni, head of spring;* cf. DlPa 261 COT:[Gn 10:12](https://ref.ly/Gen10:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 30:11; Genesis 10:12; Job 41:5; Psalms 32:9; Isaiah 30:28


## I רָסַס (id: BDB09160)

\[Str7450] † I. \[רָסַס] **vb.** moisten (so Aram. רְסַס, ܪܰܣ; Ar. رَشَّ (ش) *sprinkle*);—**Qal** *Inf. cstr.* שֶׁמֶן לָרֹס אֶת־הַסֹּלֶת Ez [46:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:14).

> \[Str7447] † I. \[רָסִיס] **n.\[m.]** drop (of dew);—pl. cstr. רְסִיסֵי לָיְלָה Ct [5:2](https://ref.ly/Song5:2) (‖טַל).

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 5:2; Ezekiel 46:14


## II רסס (id: BDB09162)

II. רסס (√ of foll.; NH רָסַס Pi. *break, crush;* so Mand. רסס NorbergLexid. 232, J Aram. רְסַס; cf. Ar. رَسٌّ *well stopped up with stones* HoffmZAW iii (1893\), 115\).

> \[Str7447] † II. \[רָסִיס] **n.\[m.]** fragment;—pl. רְסִיסִים, of houses destroyed [Am 6:11](https://ref.ly/Amos6:11) (‖בְּקִעִים).

> \[Str7451–52,7455] רַע, רֹעַ v. I. רעע. I. רֵעַ v. רוע, 947f

> \[Str7453–54] II, III. רֵעַ v. II. רעהf

* **Associated Passages:** Amos 6:11

## רָעֵב (id: BDB09166)

\[Str7456] † רָעֵב **vb.** be hungry (NH *id.* Hiph. and deriv.; As. *rûbatu, hunger;* Ar. رَغُبَ *be roomy, voracious,* رَغِبَ *desire vehemently;* Eth. ርኅበ፡ *be hungry*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ר: [Is 9:19](https://ref.ly/Isa9:19), [44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12); 3 pl. רָעֵָבוּ [Psalm 34:11](https://ref.ly/Ps34:11); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְעַב [Is 8:21](https://ref.ly/Isa8:21), 1 pl. נִרְעַב [Je 42:14](https://ref.ly/Jer42:14), etc.;—*be hungry* [Is 8:21](https://ref.ly/Isa8:21), [Gn 41:55](https://ref.ly/Gen41:55) (E; of land, \= have famine), [Je 42:14](https://ref.ly/Jer42:14) (לַלֶּחֶם), [Is 49:10](https://ref.ly/Isa49:10) (‖צמא), [65:13](https://ref.ly/Isa65:13) (‖ *id.;* opp. אכל), [Psalm 50:12](https://ref.ly/Ps50:12) *if I should be hungry* (of ´י:), [Pr 6:30](https://ref.ly/Prov6:30), [19:15](https://ref.ly/Prov19:15). **Hiph.** *allow* one *to hunger,* subj. ´י:: *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיַּרְעִבֶָךָ [Dt 8:3](https://ref.ly/Deut8:3) (opp. וַיַּאֲכִֽלְךָ), יַרְעִיב נֶפֶשׁ [Pr 10:3](https://ref.ly/Prov10:3).

> \[Str7458] רָעָב:101 **n.m.**:[Gn 26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1) famine, hunger;—abs. ´ר: [Gn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Gen12:10) \+; sf. רְעָבָם [Ne 9:15](https://ref.ly/Neh9:15); **1\.** *famine* (in land, nation or city), [Gn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Gen12:10), [26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1), [41:30](https://ref.ly/Gen41:30), [41:31](https://ref.ly/Gen41:31), [Ex 16:3](https://ref.ly/Exod16:3), [2 S 21:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:1), [24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13) \= [2 K 4:38](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:38), [6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25) \+; ´ר:(הָ )שְׁנֵי [Gn 41:27](https://ref.ly/Gen41:27), [41:30](https://ref.ly/Gen41:30), [41:36](https://ref.ly/Gen41:36), ´שְׁנַת ר: v:[50](https://ref.ly/Gen41:50), but also ´שָׁנִים ר: \[שָׁלוֺשׁ] שֶׁבֵע [2 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13) (on gender v. ## II רָעָה (id: BDB09179)

\[Str7462,7489] † II. \[רָעָה] **vb.** prob. associate with (cf. Schulthess69 ff. but also NöZMG iiv (1900\), 154 f.; As. *ru’a, ruttu, neighbour, fellow* (m. et f.; cf. JägerBAS i. 483,486\); so Bed. رَاعِىرَاعِي, راعيهاراعيها *master, owner,*, WallinZMG v (1851\), 9 JayakarJA xxi. 851; Eth. መርዓ፡ *marriage;* Ar. أُرْعُوَّةٌأَرعُت:, Eth. አርዑት፡ኡርዑዋህ፡ኡን *yoke;* NH רֵעוּה *friendship* is fr. BH רֵעַ);—**Qal** *Imv.* ms. וּרְעֵה אֱמוּנָה [Psalm 37:3](https://ref.ly/Ps37:3) *cherish faithfulness* (Hup\-Now RV Che Du Dr); \> *feed seocurely* (Bae; I.רעה; *feed on* (his) *faithfulness* Am RV); *Pt.* כְסִילִים רֹעֶה [Pr 13:20](https://ref.ly/Prov13:20) *one associating with fools,* so ´זֹלְלְים ר: [28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7), זוֺנוֺת ´ר: [29:3](https://ref.ly/Prov29:3) (Thes al. think these fig. fr. I. רעה, v. **2 b**). **Hithp.** 2 ms. אַל־תִּתְרַע אֶת־בַּעַל אָף [Pr 22:24](https://ref.ly/Prov22:24) *do not make companionship with a bad\-tempered man;* perhaps also 3 fpl. תִּתְרָעֶינָה [Is 11:7](https://ref.ly/Isa11:7) (LagOr. i. 21; Deceased Wife’s Sister 399 Brd Du Che Hpt) cow and bear *shall be each other’s companions;* \+ *Inf. cstr.* הִתְרָעֹת [Pr 18:24](https://ref.ly/Prov18:24) (Toy, for MT הִתְרֹעֵעַ) there are \[יֵשׁ, for אִישׁ] friends (merely) *to be companions* (v. II. רעע *ad fin.*).

## III רעה (id: BDB09191)

III. רעה (Aram. רְעָא, ܪܥܶܐ, *take pleasure* (in), *desire,* \= Ar. رضى \[Heb. רָחָה], whence Aram. רְעוּתָא *desire,* רַעֲיָנָא, רַעֲיוֺנָא, ܪܶܥܝܳܢܳܐ, *opinion, thought, disposition*, and following late and Aram. words in Heb., cf. NöZMG liv (1900\), 155; \> De SiegfQoh. 18 al. from I. רעה **2 b,** cf. [Ho 12:2](https://ref.ly/Hos12:2), and Ar. رَعَى; *watch, regard, respect* (Lane) \[BaWU 46 f. KauArmaismen 82 f. derive foll. from I. רעה \= *observe, attend to*, specif. *tend sheep*, also *aim at, purpose*]).

> \[Str7454] † III. \[רֵעַ] **n.\[m.]** purpose, aim;—sf. רֵעִי [Psalm 139:2](https://ref.ly/Ps139:2) (of man), pl. sf. רֵעֶיךָ v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ps139:17) (of Ēl).

> \[Str7469] † II. רְעוּת **n.f.**longing, striving;—cstr., רוּחַ ´הֶבֶל וּר: [Ec 1:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:14), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:11), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:17), [2:26](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:26), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:4), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:9); רוּחַ ´עָמָר: [4:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:6).

> \[Str7475] † רַעְיוֺן **n.\[m.]** id.;—cstr., רַעְיוֺן רוּחַ [Ec 1:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:17), רוּחַ ´הֶבֶל וְר: [4:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:16) (cf. foreg.), לִבּוֺ ´בְּכָל־עֲמָלוֺ וּבְר: [2:22](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:22).

> רֹעָה v. II. רעע.

## רָעַל (id: BDB09196)

\[Str7477,6188] † \[רָעַל] **vb.** quiver, shake, reel (Aram. רְעַל, ܪܥܶܠ, *quiver, shake, tremble;* Ar. رَعْلٌ *the hanging part* of a sheep's split ear, also (Frey) a kind of *veil* of which a part hangs down in front; أَرْعَلُ *dangling;* Liḥy. has n.pr. רעל DHMEpigr. Denkm. Ar. 38\);—**Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הַבְּרשְׁים הָרְעָלוּ [Na 2:4](https://ref.ly/Nah2:4) *are made to quiver.* Vid. also \[עָרַל].

> \[Str7478] † רַ֫עַל **n.\[m.]** reeling;—only ´סַף־ר: [Zc 12:2](https://ref.ly/Zech12:2) *goblet of reeling* (fig. of Jerusalem).

> \[Str7479] † \[רְעָלָה] **n.\[f.]** prob. veil (cf. Ar. supr.);—pl. הָֽרְעָלוֺת [Is 3:19](https://ref.ly/Isa3:19), in list of finery.

> \[Str4831] מַרְעֲלָה v. p. 599\.

> \[Str8653] † תַּרְעֵלָה **n.f.** reeling (cf. רֵ֫עַל);—´כּוֺס הַתּ: [Is 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17), [51:22](https://ref.ly/Isa51:22); ´יַיִן תּ: [Psalm 60:5](https://ref.ly/Ps60:5) drink *reeling as wine.*

> \[Str7480,7485] † רְעֵלָיָה **n.pr.m.** companion of Zerub., [Ezr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:2) (Ρεελεια, LXXL Δεμίου), \= רַעַמְיָה [Ne 7:7](https://ref.ly/Neh7:7) (Νααμια, א Δαεμια, LXXL Δαιμίας, A Ρεελμα).

## רעם (id: BDB09202)

רעם ( √ of foll., prob. onomatop.; Gerber47 prop. *move violently* as orig. meaning, but ref. to thunderous *sound* everywhere, exc. Ez [27:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:35) where text dub., v. infr.; cf. Aram. רַעַם, רְעִים n. *thunder,* רְעֵם vb. *thunder;* esp. Aph. Ithpa. *utter* (loud) *complaints;* ܪܥܶܡ, ܪܰܥܡܳܐ n. *thunder,* ܪܥܶܡ vb. *thunder, lament,* cf. NH רָעַם Hiph. *thunder,* Hithp. *complain;* Eth. ረዓም፡: n. *thunder,* As. *rîmu, id.;* Ar. رَغِمَ *vex, dislike,* etc.).

> \[Str7482] † רַ֫עַם **n.\[m.]** thunder;—ascribed to ´י:: ´ר: abs. [Is 29:6](https://ref.ly/Isa29:6), בְּסֵתֶר רַָעַם [Psalm 81:8](https://ref.ly/Ps81:8) (Baer Gi; i.e. thunder\-cloud); sf. קוֺל רַעַמְךָ [77:19](https://ref.ly/Ps77:19), [104:7](https://ref.ly/Ps104:7); cstr. רַעַם גבורתו [Jb 26:14](https://ref.ly/Job26:14) (fig. of ´י:’s display of might; opp. שֵׁמֶץ); fig. of captains, שָרִים ´ר: [39:25](https://ref.ly/Job39:25), i.e. thunderous shouting ( \+ דְּרוּעָה).

> \[Str7481] † \[רָעַם] **vb.denom.** thunder;—**Qal** *make the sound of thunder, thunder: Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְעַם *let* the sea *thunder* (in praise, ‖שמה, גיל, עלז, הריע, מחא כף, רנן) [Psalm 96:11](https://ref.ly/Ps96:11) \= [1 Ch 16:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:32), [Psalm 98:7](https://ref.ly/Ps98:7).—*Pf.* 3 pl. רָעֲמוּ פָּנִים Ez [27:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:35) is dub.: *faces tremble* (Toy *are convulsed*), or *they tremble* (cf. Hi\-Sm Krae; AV RV *are troubled*) *in face,* lacks etym. support (otherwise Gerber47, but v. √ supr.); LXX SyrVer Co דָּֽמְעוּ פְּנֵיהֶם. **Hiph.** (Gerbe47) *thunder, cause thunder;—Pf.* 3 ms. הִרְעִים [Psalm 29:3](https://ref.ly/Ps29:3); *Impf.* 3 ms. יַרְעֵם (on ֵ֯ v. Köi. 210\) [Psalm 18:14](https://ref.ly/Ps18:14) \+; וַיַּרְעֵם [1 S 7:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:10); 2ms. תַּרְעֵם [Jb 40:9](https://ref.ly/Job40:9); [1 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6) v. infr.;—*thunder,* of ´י: (God) [2:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:10), [7:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:10) (both c. עַל Pers. *against*whom), [Psalm 18:14](https://ref.ly/Ps18:14) \= [2 S 22:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:14), [Psalm 29:3](https://ref.ly/Ps29:3), [Jb 37:4](https://ref.ly/Job37:4), [37:5](https://ref.ly/Job37:5), cf. [40:9](https://ref.ly/Job40:9).—הַרְּעִמָהּ [1 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6) is appear. *Inf. cstr. sf.* (Ges§ 20 h; 22 s), but not understood by LXX and dub.: AV RV *to make her fret,* cf. Aram. *utter* (loud) *complaints* (Weir in DrSm 291\); perhaps corrupt HPS.

> \[Str7484] רַעְמָא v. II. רַעְמָה. below

> \[Str7483] † I. רַעְמָה (GiIntr. 127 f.) **n.f.** vibration? quivering mane? of horse’s neck: [Jb 39:19](https://ref.ly/Job39:19) *hast thou clothed his neck* (*with*) ´ר:? so most, but very uncertain.

> \[Str7484] † II. רַעְמָה, רַעְמָא (GiIntr. 124 ff.) **n.pr.m.** ‘son’ of Cush, הָ֯ [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) \= אָ֯ [1 Ch 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9), [1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9) (Baer, v. his note; van d. H. Gi הָ֯ vb); Ρεγχμα, Ρεγμα; הָ֯ as trading people Ez [27:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:22), Ραμα, Ραγμα. Identified by many with city Ῥεγ(α)μα (Ptolvi. 7, 14\), Ῥηγμα (Steph. Byz.), in SE. Arabia, on Pers. Gulf (so even DHMZMG xxx (1876\), 122 GlasSkizze ii.251, 325\), but this is رجمه: in inscr. (Glasib. 252\); \<Sab. רעַמה near *Me‘în* in SW. Arabia (HomSüdar. Chr. 131 Hal535, 11, cf. DHMl. c.), perh. \= Ῥαμμανῖται of Straboxvi. 4\. 24, v. Di:[Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7).

> \[Str7480,7485] רַעַמְיָה (? *thunder of* ´י:) [Ne 7:7](https://ref.ly/Neh7:7) v. רְעֵלָיָה.

> \[Str7486] † רַֽעַמְסֵס **n.pr.loc.** [Ex 1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11) (J), \= רַלְמְסֵס [Gn 47:11](https://ref.ly/Gen47:11), [Ex 12:37](https://ref.ly/Exod12:37), [Nu 33:3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3), [33:5](https://ref.ly/Num33:5) (all P), Ramses, city in Egypt; Ραμες(ς)η; built by King Rameses II (hence its name; the king used Israelitish *corveve* acc. to [Ex 1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11)), near *Tel el\-Maskhuta* (Pithom), but not certainly identified, v. פִּהֹם and reff.; ´אֶרֶץ ר: [Gn 47:11](https://ref.ly/Gen47:11) of district round.

\[Str7489,7465,7462] † II. \[רָעַע] **vb.** break (Aram. loan\-word \= Heb. רָצַץ q.v.);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. רָעוּ [Je 11:16](https://ref.ly/Jer11:16); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָרֹעַ [Je 15:12](https://ref.ly/Jer15:12), [Jb 34:24](https://ref.ly/Job34:24), 2 ms. sf. תְּרֹעֵם [Psalm 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ps2:9) (but v. infr.); *Inf. abs.* רֹ֫עָה [Is 24:19](https://ref.ly/Isa24:19) (del. ה as dittogr. and read רֹעַ, v. **Hithpō‘.**); *Pt.* f. רֹעָה (? for רֹעֲעָה) [Pr 25:19](https://ref.ly/Prov25:19):—**1\.** trans. הֲיָרֹע בַּרְזֶל ´בַּרְזֶל וגו: [Je 15:12](https://ref.ly/Jer15:12) *can one break iron, iron out of the north*? cf. יָרֹעַ כַּבִּירִים [Jb 34:24](https://ref.ly/Job34:24); תְּרֹעֵם בְּשֵֹׁבֶט בַּרְזֶל [Psalm 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ps2:9) (but rd. תִּרְעֵם, v. I. רהְ, LXX SyrVer Vulgate Hup\-Now al.). **2\.** intrans. *break \= be broken,* of branches [Je 11:16](https://ref.ly/Jer11:16) (in fig.; Gf Or al.; \> Hi Gie *are in a sad state,* √ I. רעע); שֵׁן רֹעָה [Pr 25:19](https://ref.ly/Prov25:19) *a broken tooth* (´ר: \= רֹעֲעְה?; Frankenb **Niph.** *Pt.* נְרָעָה, cf. Toy, but Niph. not elsewhere; Vrss רָעָה *bad*).—רֹ֫עוּ [Is 8:9](https://ref.ly/Isa8:9) read דְּעוּ (LXX Lo Che al., ‖הַאֲזִינוּ). **Hithpō‘.** *Pf.* 3 fs. \+ **Qal** *Inf. abs.* (v. supr.) צרץ\[הָ] רֹעַ הִתְרֹעֲעָה [Is 24:19](https://ref.ly/Isa24:19) *the earth is broken asunder* (‖פּוֺר תִתְפּוֺרְרָה, מוֺט הִתְמוֺטְטָה); also *Inf. cstr.* לְהִתְרוֺעֵעַ [Pr 18:24](https://ref.ly/Prov18:24) *will be broken in pieces* (Ges§ 114 i), *ruined,* De al., but \< read לְהִתְרָעוֺת Vrss Toy al., v. II. רעה Hithp. (see, however, DrExpos. Times xi (1899–1900\), 230f.).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 15:12; Jeremiah 11:16; Job 34:24; Isaiah 8:9; Proverbs 18:24; Proverbs 25:19; Psalms 2:9; Isaiah 24:19

## רָעַף (id: BDB09219)

\[Str7491] † \[רָעַף] **vb.** trickle, drip, synon. of נזל, II. ערף (cf. Ar. رَعَفَ *flow* (of blood), *bleed* (of the nose));—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִרְעְפוּ, of clouds [Jb 36:28](https://ref.ly/Job36:28) (c. עֲלֵי Pers.; ‖יִזְּלוּ), c. acc. mat. טַל [Pr 3:20](https://ref.ly/Prov3:20); fig. מַעְגָּלֶיךָ יִרְעֲפוּן דָּשֶׁן [Psalm 65:12](https://ref.ly/Ps65:12) (i.e. fertilizing rain); subj. of moistened ground, נְאוֺת מִדְבָּר, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ps65:13). **Hiph.** *Imv.* mpl. הַרְעִיפוּ [Is 45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8) *trickle,* O heavens, from above (fig.: ‖יִּוְּלוּ צֶרֶק).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 45:8; Psalms 65:12; Proverbs 3:20; Psalms 65:13; Job 36:28

## רָעַשׁ (id: BDB09221)

\[Str7493] † \[רָעַשׁ] **vb.** quake, shake, intr. (NH \= BH, so Aram. ܪܥܰܫ (rare), and (once) רְשְַׁ *toss, rage* (of sea); Ar. رَعَسَ, usu. رَعِشَ, رَعَشَ (loan\-word?), *tremble, quiver, quake;* cf. perhaps As. *rêšu, exult*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. רָעֲשָׁה [Je 8:16](https://ref.ly/Jer8:16), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Jer49:21), רָעָשָׁה Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4), [Psalm 68:9](https://ref.ly/Ps68:9); 3 pl. רָעֲשׁוּ [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/John2:10) \+, etc. *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְעַשׁ [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16), 3 fpl. תִּרְעַשְׁנָה Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10), etc.; *Pt.* pl. רֹעֲשִׁים [Je 4:24](https://ref.ly/Jer4:24);—*quake, shake,* of earth Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4) \= [Psalm 68:9](https://ref.ly/Ps68:9), [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) (‖רגז) \= [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) (‖ *id.*), [Psalm 77:19](https://ref.ly/Ps77:19) (\+ רנז), also [Is 13:13](https://ref.ly/Isa13:13) (c. עַל־כֵּן, \+ מִמְּקוֺמוֺ, pregn.; ‖רָגַז), [Je 51:29](https://ref.ly/Jer51:29); c. מִן caus. [Je 8:16](https://ref.ly/Jer8:16), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Jer10:10), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Jer49:21); of מוֺסְדֵי אָרֶץ [Is 24:18](https://ref.ly/Isa24:18); of heavens [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/John2:10) (לִפְנֵי caus.; ‖רָגַז); heavens and earth [4:16](https://ref.ly/John4:16); mts. [Je 4:24](https://ref.ly/Jer4:24), [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5) (מִן Pers. caus.), [Psalm 46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4) (ב caus.); walls Ez [26:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:10) (מִן caus.); אִיִּים v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:15) (id.); מִגְרשׁוֺת [27:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:28) (ל caus.); הַסִּפִּים [Am 9:1](https://ref.ly/Amos9:1); all living things Ez [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:20) (מִפָּנַי caus.); of waving grain [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16). **Niph.** נִרְעֲשָׁה הָאָרֶץ [Je 50:46](https://ref.ly/Jer50:46) *is made to quake* (מִקּוֺל). **Hiph.** *pf.* 2 ms. הִרְעַשְׁתָּה [Psalm 60:4](https://ref.ly/Ps60:4); 1 S. הַרְעַשְׁתִּי Ez [31:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:16); ´וְהִר: consec. [Hg 2:7](https://ref.ly/Hag2:7); *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. 3 ms. תַרְעִישֶׁנּוּ(הֲ) [Jb 39:20](https://ref.ly/Job39:20); *Pt.* מַרְעִישׁ [Is 14:16](https://ref.ly/Isa14:16) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *cause to quake,* subj. ´י:, c. acc. of heavens, earth, nations, etc., [Hg 2:6](https://ref.ly/Hag2:6), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hag2:7), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Hag2:21), cf. [Psalm 60:4](https://ref.ly/Ps60:4); nations \+ מִקּוֺל Ez [31:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:16); subj. man, c. acc. of kingdoms [Is 14:16](https://ref.ly/Isa14:16) (‖מַרְגִּיז). **2\.** *cause* (horse) *to spring, leap* (like locust), man subj., [Jb 39:20](https://ref.ly/Job39:20).

950:b. רַעַשׁ **1** ad fin.: del. ‘by warhorse(’s hoofs) [Jb 39:24](https://ref.ly/Job39:24) (\+ רֹגֶז)’, and **3** after ‘*quivering*’ add ‘of horse eager to start [Jb 39:24](https://ref.ly/Job39:24) 9 \+ רֹגֶז);’.

(in vision) קוֺל גָּרוֺל ´ר: [3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:12), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:13); prob. also [37:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:7) (LXX Thes Co Krae; \> *rustling, rattling,* Hi\-Sm Toy); hence hyperbol. of shaking of earth by tramping of warriors [Is 9:4](https://ref.ly/Isa9:4), by war\-chariots [Je 47:3](https://ref.ly/Jer47:3), cf. ## רָפָא (id: BDB09223)

\[Str7495] † רָפָא **vb.** heal (NH in deriv.; Ph. רפא, Syr. ܪܦܳܐ *heal;* on Aram. n.pr. cpd. with רפא v. NöZMG xi (1886\), 723 Lzb369; Sab. n.pr. אלרפא Langer1, 1 DHMZMG xxxvii (1883\), 326; Ar. رَفَأَ (and رَفَا) *darn, mend, repair, pacify;* Eth. ረፍአ፡ *stitch together, mend;* cf. KüchenmeisterZWiss. Pers. [Gn 20:17](https://ref.ly/Gen20:17) (E), [Psalm 107:20](https://ref.ly/Ps107:20) (sf.), c. ל Pers. [Nu 12:13](https://ref.ly/Num12:13) (JE) [2 K 20:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:5), [20:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:8). **b.** of men, abs. [Ec 3:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:3) (opp. הרג); pt. as subst. *healer, physician,* [Gn 50:2](https://ref.ly/Gen50:2), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Gen50:2) (J) [2 Ch 16:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:12). **2\.** fig., *heal* hurts of nation, involving ´י:’s (restored) favour (and, often, forgiveness): **a.** subj. ´י:, c. sf. Pers., [Ho 6:1](https://ref.ly/Hos6:1), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Hos11:3) (yet cf. We Now), [Ex 15:26](https://ref.ly/Exod15:26) (J), [Is 19:22](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22) b, [57:18](https://ref.ly/Isa57:18), [57:19](https://ref.ly/Isa57:19), [Je 33:6](https://ref.ly/Jer33:6), [Psalm 30:3](https://ref.ly/Ps30:3), cf. [6:3](https://ref.ly/Ps6:3); c. acc. עַם [2 Ch 30:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:20), c. acc. אֶרֶץ [2 Ch 7:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:14); c. ל of nation [Ho 7:1](https://ref.ly/Hos7:1), and (indef. subj.) [Is 6:10](https://ref.ly/Isa6:10) (lest) *one heal them* (\= pass., *they be healed*); c. acc. of hurt, מִחַץ מַכָּתוֺ [30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26), שְׁבָרִים (of land) [Psalm 60:4](https://ref.ly/Ps60:4), so סְשׁוּבָה [Ho 14:5](https://ref.ly/Hos14:5), [Je 3:22](https://ref.ly/Jer3:22) (incl. the *consequences* of backsliding); c. ל of hurt [Psalm 103:3](https://ref.ly/Ps103:3); acc. nation (personif.) \+ מִן of hurt [Je 30:17](https://ref.ly/Jer30:17) *I will heal thee of thy wounds* (‖אֶרְפָּאֵח); abs. [Is 19:22a](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22) (opp. נגף), [Dt 32:39](https://ref.ly/Deut32:39) (opp. מחץ); pt. as subst. *healer, physician* [Je 8:22](https://ref.ly/Jer8:22). **b.** human subj., c. ל of nation, [Ho 5:13](https://ref.ly/Hos5:13), cf. [La 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lam2:13). **3\. a.** fig., *heal* individ. distresses, c. sf. Pers. [Je 17:14](https://ref.ly/Jer17:14), c. acc. נַפְשִׁי \= *me* [41:5](https://ref.ly/Jer41:5), c. ל Pers., לִשְׁבוּרֵי לֵב [Psalm 147:3](https://ref.ly/Ps147:3) (i.e. longing exiles, cf. [Is 61:3](https://ref.ly/Isa61:3)); abs. [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18) (opp. מחץ); pt. as subst. [Jb 13:4](https://ref.ly/Job13:4). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִרְפָּא [Lv 13:37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37) \+, 3 fs. נִרְפָּתָה (Ges§ 75 qq) [Je 51:9](https://ref.ly/Jer51:9); 3 pl. וְנִרְפּאו consec. Ez [47:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:8); *Impf.* 2 ms. תֵּרָפֵא [Je 51:8](https://ref.ly/Jer51:8); 3 mpl. יֵרָֽפְאוּ Ez [47:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:9), [47:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:11), וַיֵּרָפו (Gesl.c.) [2 K 2:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:22), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* הֵרָפֵא [Je 15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18) \+, הֵרָפֵה (Idib.) [Je 19:11](https://ref.ly/Jer19:11);—*be healed:* **1\.** lit., of Pers. [1 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:3); \+ acc. of disease [Dt 28:27](https://ref.ly/Deut28:27), [28:35](https://ref.ly/Deut28:35); subj. disease [Lv 13:18](https://ref.ly/Lev13:18), [13:37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37), [14:3](https://ref.ly/Lev14:3), [14:48](https://ref.ly/Lev14:48); of (bad) water [2 K 2:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:22), salt waters (prediction), i.e. be made fresh, Ez [47:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:8), [47:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:9), [47:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:11); of (broken) pottery, i.e. be made whole, [Je 19:11](https://ref.ly/Jer19:11) (in sim.). **2\.** fig., *be healed:* **a.** of national hurts, subj. city [Je 51:8](https://ref.ly/Jer51:8), [51:9](https://ref.ly/Jer51:9); involving forgiveness and ´י:’s blessing, impers. c. ל of people, נִרְפָּא־לָנוּ [Is 53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5) i.e. *healing has come to us.* **b.** of personal distress, subj. Pers. [Je 17:14](https://ref.ly/Jer17:14), subj. the distress [15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 S. רִפִּאתִי [2 K 2:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:21); 2 mpl. רְפֵּאתֶם Ez [34:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:4); 1 pl. רִפִּאנוּ [Je 51:9](https://ref.ly/Jer51:9); *Impf.* 3 ms. יְרַפֵּא [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19), [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16), וַיְרַפֵּא [1 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:30); 3 mpl. וַיְרַפְּאוּ [Je 6:14](https://ref.ly/Jer6:14), וַיְרַפּוּ (Ges§ 75 qq) [8:11](https://ref.ly/Jer8:11); *Inf. abs.* רַפֹּא [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19);—*have healed, heal,* usu. human subj.: **1\.** lit., רַפּאֹ יְרַפֵּא [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19) *he shall have* (*him*) *well healed;* c. acc. of altar, \= *repair,* [1 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:30); c. ל of water (subj. ´י:) [2 K 2:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:21). **2\.** fig. of healing national defects and hurts, acc. Pers., Ez [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14), [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16); *work at healing, treat* (acc. of hurt) [Je 6:14](https://ref.ly/Jer6:14), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Jer8:11) (both עַל־נְקַלָּה, v. \[קָלַל] **Niph. 2**), [51:9](https://ref.ly/Jer51:9). **Hithp.** *Inf. cstr.* of purpose, לְהִתְרַפֵּא, lit., *in order to get healed,* \+ מִן of wounds [2 K 8:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:29) \= [9:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:15), so ‖ [2 Ch 22:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:6) (rd. with LXX מִן for כִּי, cf. Be Kau Benz).

> \[Str7509] † רְפָיָה **n.pr.m. 1\.** in David’s family [1 Ch 3:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:21), Ραφαλ, A LXXL Ραφαια. **2\.** in Simeon, [4:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:42), Ραφαια(ς). **3\.** in Issachar, [7:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:2), Ραφαρα, A LXXL Ραφαια. [4](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:4). descendant of Saul [9:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:43), Ραφαια, LXXL Αραχα (\= רָפָה [8:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:37)). **5\.** post\-ex. name [Ne 3:9](https://ref.ly/Neh3:9), Ραφαια(ς).

> \[Str4832] † מַרְפֵּא, מַרְפֵּה **n.m.**:[Ec 10:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:4) healing, cure, health (usu. fig.);—abs. מַרְפֵּא [Je 14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19) \+, מַרְפֵּה [8:15](https://ref.ly/Jer8:15); cstr. מַרְפֵּא [Pr 15:4](https://ref.ly/Prov15:4);—**1\.** *healing, cure,* of national woes, [Je 8:15](https://ref.ly/Jer8:15), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19) b (‖שָׁלוֺם), [36:6](https://ref.ly/Jer36:6) (\+ אֲרֻכָה); esp. ´אֵין לָנוּ מ: [Je 14:19a](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19) *there is no cure for us,* ´עַר לְאֵין מ: [2 Ch 36:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:16). **2\.** fig. of Pers., *health, profit,* [Pr 4:22](https://ref.ly/Prov4:22) (‖הַּיִּים), [12:18](https://ref.ly/Prov12:18), [13:17](https://ref.ly/Prov13:17), [16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24); with spiritual implic. [Mal 3:20](https://ref.ly/Mal3:20); ´אֵין מ: [Pr 6:15](https://ref.ly/Prov6:15) \= [29:1](https://ref.ly/Prov29:1); ´לֵב מ: [14:30](https://ref.ly/Prov14:30) *a mind of health* (healthy, composed, mind), cf. ´מ: alone \= *composure* [Ec 10:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:4); לְשׁוֺן ´מ: [Pr 15:4](https://ref.ly/Prov15:4) *healing of the tongue,* \= a soothing tongue (Toy). **3\.** lit., of disease, ´לְאֵין מ: [2 Ch 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:18).

## רָפַד (id: BDB09235)

\[Str7502] † \[רָפַד] **vb.** spread (As. *rapâdu, stretch oneself;* Ar. رَفَدَ *prop up, support, aid, give,* cf. **Pi.** infr.; Sab. רפדת pl. *protection, guard,* CISiv, no. 40, 3, cf. ארפד *terraces* DHMHofmus.);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְפַּד [Jb 41:22](https://ref.ly/Job41:22) *he* (the crocodile) *spreadeth a threshing\-sledge upon the mud* (leaves marks upon it from his scales). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 S. of couch (fig.) רִפַּדְתּי יְצוּעָי [Jb 17:13](https://ref.ly/Job17:13); then (*spread out* any support, hence) *Imv.* mpl. sf. רַפְּדוּנִי Ct [2:5](https://ref.ly/Song2:5) *support me* with apples (‖סַמְּכוּ).

> \[Str7507] † \[רְפִידָה] **n.f.** perh. support (cf. Ar. supr.), i.e. back, or arm, of palanquin; sf. רְפִידָתוֺ Ct [3:10](https://ref.ly/Song3:10); LXX ἀνάΚλιτον, Vulgate *reclinatorium.*

> \[Str7508,774] † רְפִידִים **n.pr.loc.** station of Isr. at Exodus (in P), [Ex 17:1](https://ref.ly/Exod17:1), 19:2; דְפִּידִם [17:8](https://ref.ly/Exod17:8), [Nu 33:14](https://ref.ly/Num33:14), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Num33:15); Ραφιδειν.—אַרְפַּד v. p. 75\.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 41:22; Job 17:13; Numbers 33:15; Song of Songs 3:10; Exodus 17:1; Numbers 33:14; Song of Songs 2:5; Exodus 17:8


## רָפה (id: BDB09238)

\[Str7503] † רָפה **vb.** sink, relax (NH *id.,* so Aram. רפא (der. spec.), Zinj. רפה Aph., ܪܦܳܐ; Ar. رَفَاهَة *an easy life* is perhaps akin);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. Ju [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9) (yet v. infr.); 3 fs. רָֽפִָתָה Ju [8:3](https://ref.ly/Judg8:3), [Je 49:24](https://ref.ly/Jer49:24), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְפֶּה [Is 5:24](https://ref.ly/Isa5:24), וַיִּרֶף [Ex 4:26](https://ref.ly/Exod4:26) (v. infr.), etc.;—**1\.** *sink down,* of hay in flame [Is 5:24](https://ref.ly/Isa5:24); *sink, decline,* of day Ju [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9) (si vera l.; LXXL ΚέΚλιΚεν, GFM נָטָה \[but how expl. the difficult רפה?]). Usu. **2\.** *sink, drop* (of wings, v. **Pi.**), of hands, c. מִן [Ne 6:9](https://ref.ly/Neh6:9) *their hands will drop from the work* (in fear); elsewh. abs. fig. \= *lose heart, energy,* [2 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:1), [Is 13:7](https://ref.ly/Isa13:7), [Je 6:24](https://ref.ly/Jer6:24), [50:43](https://ref.ly/Jer50:43), Ez [7:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:17), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12), [Zp 3:16](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:16); יָרַיִם om. [Je 49:24](https://ref.ly/Jer49:24). **3\.** *sink, relax, abate,* of temper Ju [8:3](https://ref.ly/Judg8:3) (\+ מֵעַל Pers.). **4\.** *relax, withdraw,* subj. ´י:, מִן Pers. [Ex 4:26](https://ref.ly/Exod4:26) (J), i.e. let one alone (but rd. perh. וַיֶרֶף, v. **Hiph.**). **Niph.** *Pt.* pl. נִרְפִּים *idle* [Ex 5:8](https://ref.ly/Exod5:8), [5:17](https://ref.ly/Exod5:17) (J). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. רִפָּה [Jb 12:21](https://ref.ly/Job12:21); *Impf.* 3 fpl. תְּרַפֶּינָה Ez [1:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:24), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:25); *Pt.* מְרַפֵּא (Ges§ 75 rr) [Je 38:4](https://ref.ly/Jer38:4); pl. מְפִַּים [Ezr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:4);—causat.: *let* wings *drop* Ez [1:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:24), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:25) (rd. **Qal,** כנפיהם subj., LXX Co Toy Krae al., who del. in v:[25](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:25)); loosen and *let drop,* girdle of mighty, fig. for *weaken* them [Jb 12:21](https://ref.ly/Job12:21); c. acc. hands, fig. for *enfeeble, dishearten* [Je 38:4](https://ref.ly/Jer38:4), [Ezr 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:4). **Hiph.** *Impf.,* 3 ms. sf. יַרְפְּךָ [Dt 4:31](https://ref.ly/Deut4:31) \+; juss. 2 ms. תֶּ֫רֶף [Jos 10:6](https://ref.ly/Josh10:6) \+, etc.; *Imv.* ms. הַרְפֵּה Ju [11:37](https://ref.ly/Judg11:37), [2 K 4:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:27); הֶֶ֫רֶךָ [Dt 9:14](https://ref.ly/Deut9:14) \+; mpl. הַרְפּוּ [Psalm 46:11](https://ref.ly/Ps46:11);—causat., **1\.** *let drop,* the hand [2 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:16) \= [1 Ch 21:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:15); fig., \+ מִן Pers., \= *abandon,* [Jos 10:6](https://ref.ly/Josh10:6) (JE); Perh. also (יָד om.) \= *relax, refrain* [Ex 4:26](https://ref.ly/Exod4:26) (rdg. וַיֶרֶף, v. **Qal**); acc. rei, \= *abandon, forsake,* [Ne 6:3](https://ref.ly/Neh6:3), so (subj. ´י:) [Psalm 138:8](https://ref.ly/Ps138:8); subj. ´י:, c. acc. Pers., [Dt 4:31](https://ref.ly/Deut4:31), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Deut31:6), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Deut31:8), [Jos 1:5](https://ref.ly/Josh1:5) (D), [1 Ch 28:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:20). **2\.** *let go,* sf. Pers., Ct [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4) (opp. אחז); fig., acc. (om.) מוּסָר, [Pr 4:13](https://ref.ly/Prov4:13) (opp. החזיק), צְדָקָה [Jb 27:6](https://ref.ly/Job27:6) (opp. *id.*). **3\.** *refrain,* c. מִן Pers., \= *let* one *alone* Ju [11:37](https://ref.ly/Judg11:37), [Dt 9:14](https://ref.ly/Deut9:14), מִן of anger [Psalm 37:8](https://ref.ly/Ps37:8); c. ל Pers. [1 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:3), [2 K 4:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:27); acc. Pers. [Jb 7:19](https://ref.ly/Job7:19); abs. *let alone,* i.e. do nothing, be quiet, [1 S 15:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:16), [Psalm 46:11](https://ref.ly/Ps46:11) (cf. Weir in Dr:[1 S 15:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:16)).—[Je 3:22](https://ref.ly/Jer3:22), [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18), [Psalm 60:4](https://ref.ly/Ps60:4) v. רָפָא. **Hithp.** *Pf.* 2 ms. הִתְרַפִּיתָ [Pr 24:10](https://ref.ly/Prov24:10) *hast shewn thyself slack; Pt.* מִתְרַפֶּה [18:9](https://ref.ly/Prov18:9) *one shewing himself slack,* pl. מִתְרַפִּים [Jos 18:3](https://ref.ly/Josh18:3).—Vid. also רָפָא *heal.*

> \[Str7504,7503] † רָפֶה **adj.** slack;—abs. ´ר:, of people, [Nu 13:18](https://ref.ly/Num13:18) (J); cstr. רְפֵה יָרַיִם [2 S 17:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:2), i.e. weak (‖יָגֵעַ); fpl. יָרַיִם רָפוֺת, fig. of discouragement and fear [Is 35:3](https://ref.ly/Isa35:3), [Jb 4:3](https://ref.ly/Job4:3). Cf. √ **Qal 2\.** p. 951

> \[Str7498] † II. רָפָה, II. רָפָא as **n.pr.m.,** alw. c. art. Hā\-Rāphā (perh. der. fr. n.pr.gent. II. רְפָאִים, q.v.);—הָֽרפָה [2 S 21:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:16), [21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18), c. prep. ´לְהָ: v:[20](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:20), v:[21:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:22) \= לְהָרָפָא [1 Ch 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:6), [20:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:8);—only as parent, ´בִּילִידֵי הָר: [2 S 21:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:16), [21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18) (\= מִילִירֵי הָֽרְפָאִים [1 Ch 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:4)); ´יֻלַּד לְהָר: v:[20](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:20), v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:22) ‖ [1 Ch 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:6), [20:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:8).—I. רָפָה v. sub רפָא. p. 951

Lit. and late, name of dead in Shₑ’ôl, c. art. ´הָר: [Jb 26:5](https://ref.ly/Job26:5), elsewhere nearly \= n.pr.: ´ר: [Is 14:9](https://ref.ly/Isa14:9) (in Shₑ’ôl), [26:4](https://ref.ly/Isa26:4) (‖מֵתִים), [Psalm 88:11](https://ref.ly/Ps88:11) (‖ *id.*), [Pr 2:18](https://ref.ly/Prov2:18) (‖מָוֶוֶת), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Prov9:18) (in עִמְקֵי שְׁאוֺל), קְהַל רְפָאִים [21:16](https://ref.ly/Prov21:16); of righteous Isr. ## רָפַס (id: BDB09247)

\[Str7511,7515] † רָפַס, רָפַש **vb.** stamp, tread, foul by stamping, treading (NH רָפַס *tread,* Syr. ܪܦܰܣ; cf. Ar. رَفَسَ *kick*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms. וַתִּרְפֹּס Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2), c. acc. rivers; 2 mpl. תִּרְפּשָֹׁוּן [34:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:18). **Niph.** *Pt.* מַעְיָן נִרְפָּש [Pr 25:26](https://ref.ly/Prov25:26) *a fountain befouled.* **Hithp.** (*stamp oneself down,* then fig.), *Imv.* ms. הִתְרַפֵּס [Pr 6:3](https://ref.ly/Prov6:3) *humble thyself,* become a suppliant (RVm Toy *bestir thyself,* as conject.); *Pt.* מִתְרַפֵּס בְּרַצֵּי כָסֶף [Psalm 68:31](https://ref.ly/Ps68:31) *stamping, trampling, down pieces of silver* (?), obscure and prob. crpt., see conjj. in Bae al. and פַּתְרֹס supr., ad fin.

> \[Str4833] † \[מַרְפֵּש?] **n.\[m]** (water) befouled (by trampling);—cstr. מִרְפַּש רַגְלֵיכֶם Ez [34:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:19).

> \[Str7513] † \[רפְסֹדָה] **n.\[f.]** raft (etym. dub.; NH once רַפְסוּרוֺת *bench* or *stool* on which feet rest);—pl. רַפְסֹדוֺת [2 Ch 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:16) (late ‖ for דֹּבְרוֺת [1 K 5:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 6:3; Ezekiel 32:2; Psalms 68:31; 1 Kings 5:23; Proverbs 25:26; 2 Chronicles 2:16; Ezekiel 34:19; Ezekiel 34:18


## רָפַף (id: BDB09250)

\[Str7322] † \[רָפַף] **vb. Pō‘.** shake, rock (Aram. רְפַף, ܪܰܦ *move gently;* Ar. رَفَّ *quiver, flash, throb;* also NH רִפְרֵף *flutter,* Ar. رَفْرَفَ *flutter, flap wings*);—*Impf.* 3 mpl. עַמּוּדֵי שָׁמַיִם יְרוֺפָפוּ [Jb 26:11](https://ref.ly/Job26:11).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 26:11


## רָפַק (id: BDB09251)

\[Str7514] † \[רָפַק] **vb. Hithp.** support oneself, lean (Eth. ረፈቀ፡ *reclinare, accumbere* ad mensam; cf. Ar. رَفَقَ *be gentle, bind camel’s arm to prevent going quickly;* مَرْفِقٌ, NH מַרְפַּק, J Aram. מַרְפְּקָא, *elbow*);—*Pt.* f. מִתְרַפֶּקֶת Ct [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5) (c. עַל Pers.).

> \[Str7511,7515] רפש v. רפס. above

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 8:5
* **Associated Articles:** רָפַס (ID: `BDB09247@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## רפשׁ (id: BDB09253)

רפשׁ (√ of foll.; NH רֶפֶש \= BH; Schulthess71 f. cp. Ar. رَفَثَ *talk* or *act obscenely,* and a Syr. ܪܶܦܬܶܐ *slag, refuse* (not in PS Brock al.)).

> \[Str7516] † רֶ֫פֶשׁ **n.\[m.]** mire;—[Is 57:20](https://ref.ly/Isa57:20) (\+ טִיט).

> \[Str7517] † \[רֶ֫פֶת] **n.\[m.]** appar. stable, stall (NH *id.;* √ dub.; Thes fr. רפף, cf. Ar. رَفٌّ *enclosure for sheep and goats,* but no satisfactory meaning of רפף known);—pl. רְפָתִים Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17) (for cattle).

> \[Str7323,7518] רָץ v. רוץ. \[רוץ] v. רצץ

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 57:20; Habakkuk 3:17
* **Associated Articles:** רוּץ (ID: `BDB08988@BDBHebrewLexicon`); רָצַץ (ID: `BDB09276@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## רָצָא (id: BDB09257)

\[Str7519,7521] † \[רָצָא] **vb.** si vera l., ‖ form of רוּץ *run, Inf. abs.* רָצוֺא Ez [1:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:14), but rd. יָצוֺא (יֵֽצְאוּ Hi\-Sm Co Toy, cf. Krae Berthol al.; LXX om. Verse and most mod. think interpol.—Vid. also רצה. p. 953

## רָצַד (id: BDB09258)

\[Str7520] † \[רָצַד] **vb. Pi.** watch stealthily, or with envious hostility (Ar. رَصَدَ *watch* or *wait* (oft. *lie in wait*) *for;* ירצר [Ecclus 14:22](https://ref.ly/Sir14:22) *observe stealthily,* so once Aram. רְצַר LevyNHWB iv. 464\);—only *Impf.* 2 mpl. תְּרַצְּדוּן [Psalm 68:17](https://ref.ly/Ps68:17) (fig. of mts., c. acc.).

## רָקַח (id: BDB09285)

\[Str7543] † \[רָקַח] **vb.** mix, or compound oil, ointment (perh. denom.; in this case √ meaning unknown; NH has מִרְקַחַת *ointment;* Ph. רקח either *spice\-mixer* (Bloch) or *spice\-dealer* (Lzb); perh. akin to As. *riḳḳu, spice,* MeissnSuppl. 90, cf. *rikkê*, DlHWB 620\);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִרְקַח [Ex 30:33](https://ref.ly/Exod30:33); *Pt.* רֹקֵחַ v:[25](https://ref.ly/Exod30:25), v:[35](https://ref.ly/Exod30:35), ´רוֺ: [37:29](https://ref.ly/Exod37:29), [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1); pl. cstr. רֹקְחֵי [1 Ch 9:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:39);—*mix, compound,* [Ex 30:33](https://ref.ly/Exod30:33) (P; כָּמֹהוּ, i.e. anything like the holy oil); pt. as subst. הַמִּרְקַחַת רֹקְחֵי [1 Ch 9:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:30) *compounders of the ointment;* as a professional name, *mixer, perfumer,* [Ex 30:25](https://ref.ly/Exod30:25), [30:35](https://ref.ly/Exod30:35), [37:29](https://ref.ly/Exod37:29), [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1). **Pu.** *Pt.* mpl. מְרֻקָּחִים [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14) *mixed as ointment.* **Hiph.** *Inf. abs.* הַרְקַח הַמֶּרְקָחָה Ez [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:10) (‖הָתֵם), as Imv., *spice the spicing* (?), i.e. spice (the meat) well, but very dub., Krae prop. חָרֵק הַמָּרָק *empty out the broth.*

> \[Str7544] † רֶ֫קַח **n.\[m.]** spice;—´יַיִן הָר: Ct [8:2](https://ref.ly/Song8:2) wine, (that is) *spice* (i.e. spiced wine).

> \[Str7545] † רֹ֫קֵחַ **n.\[m.]** spice\-mixture, perfume;—of holy oil, מִרְקַחַת מַעֲשֵה רֹקֵחַ ´ר: [Ex 30:25](https://ref.ly/Exod30:25); of incense, מַעֲשֵה רוֺקֵחַ ´ר: v:[35](https://ref.ly/Exod30:35).

> \[Str7546] † \[רַקָּח] **n. m.** ointment\-maker, perfumer;—only in phr. בֶּן־הָרַקָּחִים [Ne 3:8](https://ref.ly/Neh3:8), i.e. one of the perfumers, i.e. of that guild.

> \[Str7548] † \[רקָּחָה] **n.f.** (female) ointment\-maker, perfumer;—pl. רַקָּחוֺת [1 S 8:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:13) (\+ טַבָּחוֺת, אֹפוֺת).

> \[Str7547] † \[רִקּוּחַ] **n.\[m.]** perfumery;—pl. sf. רִקֻּחָיִח [Is 57:9](https://ref.ly/Isa57:9) *thy perfumeries, unguents.*

> \[Str4840,4026] † \[מֶרְקָח] **n.\[m.]** spice, perfume (of cheeks); pl. מֶרְקָחִים Ct [5:13](https://ref.ly/Song5:13) (v. also מִגְדָּל **3**). p. 153

> \[Str4841] † מֶרְקָחָה **n.f. 1\.** ointment\-pot, in which ointment is compounded, [Jb 41:23](https://ref.ly/Job41:23) (fig.). **2\.** spice\-seasoning (?); as acc. cogn. v. √ **Hiph.**

> \[Str4842] † מִרְקַ֫חַת **n.f. 1\.** ointment\-mixture. **2\.** ointment\-pot (?);—**1\.** abs. ´רֹקְחֵי הַמּ: [1 Ch 9:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:30); ´רֹקַח מ: [Ex 30:25](https://ref.ly/Exod30:25). **2\.** ´בְּמ: [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14) *in an ointment\-pot* (?), or *as an ointment\-mixture.*

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 9:39; 1 Chronicles 9:30; Ecclesiastes 10:1; Exodus 30:25; Job 41:23; Ezekiel 24:10; 1 Samuel 8:13; Exodus 30:33; Song of Songs 8:2; Nehemiah 3:8; Song of Songs 5:13; Isaiah 57:9; Exodus 30:35; 2 Chronicles 16:14; Exodus 37:29


## רָקַם (id: BDB09294)

\[Str7551] † \[רָקַם] **vb.** variegate (NH *id.;* so Ar. رَقَمَ, Eth. ረቀመ፡, cf. Targum רְקָם, רִקְמָתָא *variegated cloth* or *skin,* Syr. ܬܰܪܩܡܳܬܳܐ *freckles*);—**Qal** *Pt. act.* רֹקֵם, as subst. *variegator,* worker (weaver) in colours, c. ב mater. [Ex 38:23](https://ref.ly/Exod38:23), ´מְלֶאכֶת חָרָשׁ וְחשֵׁב וְר: [35:35](https://ref.ly/Exod35:35); esp. ´מַעֲשֵה ר: *variegator’s work* [26:36](https://ref.ly/Exod26:36), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Exod27:16), [28:39](https://ref.ly/Exod28:39) \= [36:37](https://ref.ly/Exod36:37), [38:18](https://ref.ly/Exod38:18), [39:29](https://ref.ly/Exod39:29) (all P; all c. mater. exc. [28:39](https://ref.ly/Exod28:39)). **Pu.** *Pf.* 1 S. רֻקַּמְתִּי [Psalm 139:15](https://ref.ly/Ps139:15) *I was skilfully wrought* (woven, ‖סכד v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ps139:13)).

> \[Str7553] † רִקְמָה **n.f.** variegated stuff (woven or embroidered);—abs. ´ר: Ju [5:30](https://ref.ly/Judg5:30) \+; sf. רִקְמָתֵח Ez [16:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:18), תָם\- [26:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:16); du. רִקְמָתַיִם Ju [5:30](https://ref.ly/Judg5:30); pl. רְקָמוֺת [Psalm 45:15](https://ref.ly/Ps45:15);—(piece of) *variegated stuff,* Ju [5:30](https://ref.ly/Judg5:30), [5:30](https://ref.ly/Judg5:30), for garments Ez [16:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:10), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:13), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16), [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24), [Psalm 45:15](https://ref.ly/Ps45:15); explicitly, ´בִּגְדֵי ר: Ez [16:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:18), [26:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:16); ´ר: appar. \= *variegated work* [27:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:7); fig. of variegated plumage [17:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:3); of variegated stone [1 Ch 29:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:2).

> \[Str7552] † רֶ֫קֶם **n.pr. 1\. m. a.** a king of Midian [Nu 31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8) (ΡοΚομ), [Jos 13:21](https://ref.ly/Josh13:21) (Ροβοκ, A Ροκομ, LXX ΟροΚομ). **b.** a ‘son’ of Hebron [1 Ch 2:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:43); [2:44](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:44), Ρεκομ, Ροκομ, Ρωκημ. **c.** name in Gilead [1 Ch 7:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:16), LXXL Ρακαμ. **2\. loc.** in Benj., [Jos 18:27](https://ref.ly/Josh18:27), Νακαν, A LXXL Ρεκεμ\[ν].

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 2:44; 1 Chronicles 7:16; Exodus 35:35; Joshua 13:21; Ezekiel 26:16; Exodus 28:39; Ezekiel 17:3; Judges 5:30; Exodus 39:29; Ezekiel 27:24; Psalms 45:15; Exodus 38:18; Exodus 36:37; 1 Chronicles 29:2; Joshua 18:27; Numbers 31:8; Exodus 26:36; Ezekiel 16:13; 1 Chronicles 2:43; Ezekiel 16:18; Exodus 27:16; Ezekiel 16:10; Ezekiel 27:16; Psalms 139:13; Ezekiel 27:7; Psalms 139:15; Exodus 38:23


## רָקַע (id: BDB09297)

\[Str7554] † \[רָקַע] **vb.** beat, stamp, beat out, spread out (NH *id.;* Hiph., Aram. רְקַע Aph., both *spread out,* and deriv.; Syr. ܪܩܰܥ *press down* ([Lk 6:38](https://ref.ly/Luke6:38) SyrVer), *spread out,* also *consolidate;* Ar. رَقَعَ *patch, put on a patch, repair,* cf. Chr.—Pal. ܡܪܩܥܐ *patch,* ܡܪܘܩܥܢܝܬܐ *swaddling\-bands,* SchwallyIdiot. 90; Ph. מרקע *platter,* or *bowl,* of gold CISi. 90, 1\);—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 S. sf. אֶרְקָעֵם [2 S 22:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:43) *I will stamp them down* (prob. gloss to אֲדִקֵּם preceding, of. Bae:[Psalm 18:43](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43)); *Imv.* רְקַע בְּדַגְלְךָ Ez [6:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:11) *beat* (stamp) with thy foot, in token of contemptuous pleasure, cf. Da (‖הַכֵּה בְכַפְּךָ); so *Inf. cstr.* sf. רַקְעֲךָ בְּרָגֶל [25:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:6) (‖מַחְאֲךָ יָד); *Pt. act.* as subst. cstr. (Ges§ 65 d) רֹקַע הָאָרֶץ (i.e. ´י:) *he that* (beateth out) *spreadeth out the earth* [Is 42:5](https://ref.ly/Isa42:5), רֹקַע ´הַא: [44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24), ´רוֺקַע הָא: [Psalm 136:6](https://ref.ly/Ps136:6). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. בַּזָּהָב יְרַקְּעֶנּוּ [Is 40:19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19) a goldsmith *with gold over\-layeth it* (lit. *beateth it out* 3 mpl. וַיְרַקְּעוּ אֶ־ת פַּחֵי הַזָּהָב [Ex 39:3](https://ref.ly/Exod39:3) *they beat out the plates of gold;* sf. וַיְרִקְּעוּם חִפּוּי [Nu 17:4](https://ref.ly/Num17:4) *they beat them out as plating.* **Pu.** *Pt.* כֶּסֶף מְרֻקָּע [Je 10:9](https://ref.ly/Jer10:9) *silver beaten out.* **Hiph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. תַּרְקִיעַ עִמּוֺ לִשְׁחָקִים [Jb 37:18](https://ref.ly/Job37:18) *canst thou make with* (\= like) *him a spreading for clouds* (spread out clouds; cf, רָקִיעַ)?

> \[Str7549] †רָקִיעַ **n.m.**:[Gn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Gen1:6) extended surface, (solid) expanse (as if *beaten out;* cf. [Jb 37:18](https://ref.ly/Job37:18));—abs. ´ר: Ez [1:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:22) \+, cstr. ´רְ: [Gn 1:14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14) \+;—LXX στερέωμα, Vulgate *firmamentum,* cf. Syr. sub √supr.;—**1\.** (flat) *expanse* (as if of ice, cf. כְּעֵין הַקֶּרַח), as base, support (WklAltor. Forsch. iv. 347\) Ez [1:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:22), [1:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:23), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:25) (gloss ? cf. Co Toy), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:26) (supporting ´י:’s throne). Hence (CoEz [1:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:22)) **2\.** the vault of heaven, or ‘firmament,’ regarded by Hebrews as solid, and supporting ‘waters’ above it, [Gn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Gen1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Gen1:8) (called שָׁמַיַם; all P), [Psalm 19:2](https://ref.ly/Ps19:2) (‖הַשָּׁמַיַם), ´זֹהַר הָר: [Dn 12:3](https://ref.ly/Dan12:3); also ´ר: הַשָּׁמִיִם [Gn 1:14](https://ref.ly/Gen1:14), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Gen1:15), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Gen1:17), ´הַשּׁ :´עַל־פְּנֵי ר: v:[20](https://ref.ly/Gen1:20) (all P).
> 
> 956:a. רָקִיעַ **2\.** add ‘רְקִיעַ עֻזּוֺ [Psalm 150:1](https://ref.ly/Ps150:1) (sf. ref. to ´י:).’

> \[Str7555] †\[רִקּוּעַ] **n.\[m.]** expansion;—pl. cstr. וְעָשוּ אֹתָם רִקֻּעֵי פַחִים [Nu 17:3](https://ref.ly/Num17:3) *expansions of plates,* i.e. they shall be beaten into *broad plates.*

## I רקק (id: BDB09300)

I. רקק (√of foll.; Ar. رَقَّ *be thin,* also fig. *be weak, slender, scanty,* etc.; Eth. ረቀቀ፡ *be thin,* ረቂቅ፡ *thin;* Syr. ܪܰܩ Pa. Aph. *make thin,* ܪܰܩܺܝܩ *thin*).

> \[Str7534–35,7535] רַק †**1\. adj.** thin;—fpl. רַקּוֺת, of kine, [Gn 41:19](https://ref.ly/Gen41:19), [41:20](https://ref.ly/Gen41:20), [41:27](https://ref.ly/Gen41:27) (‖ v:[3](https://ref.ly/Gen41:3); v:[4](https://ref.ly/Gen41:4) דַּקּוֺת). **2\.**:109 **adv.** with restrictive force, only, altogether, surely (syn. אַח);—**a.** *only,* [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24), [41:40](https://ref.ly/Gen41:40) רַק הַכִּסֵּא אֶגְדַּל מִמֶּךָּ *only* as regards the throne, etc., [47:22](https://ref.ly/Gen47:22) רַק אַדְמַת הַכֹּהֲנִים לאֹ קָנָה, [50:8](https://ref.ly/Gen50:8), [Ex 8:5](https://ref.ly/Exod8:5) רַק בַּיְאֹר תִּשָּׁאַרְנָה, [10:17](https://ref.ly/Exod10:17) וְיָסֵר מֵעָלַי רַק הַמָּוֶת הַזֶּה *only* this death, [Dt 2:35](https://ref.ly/Deut2:35) (cf. [20:14](https://ref.ly/Deut20:14), [Jos 8:2](https://ref.ly/Josh8:2), [8:27](https://ref.ly/Josh8:27)), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Josh3:11), Ju [6:39](https://ref.ly/Judg6:39) (cf. אַח *ib.* [Gn 18:32](https://ref.ly/Gen18:32), [Ex 10:17](https://ref.ly/Exod10:17)), [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34) (circ. cl.) רַק הִיא יְחִידָה (there being) *only* she, an only one, [1 S 1:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:13), [Am 3:2](https://ref.ly/Amos3:2) רק אתכם ידעתי *only* you have I known, etc., [Jb 1:15](https://ref.ly/Job1:15), [1:16](https://ref.ly/Job1:16), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Job1:17), [1:19](https://ref.ly/Job1:19) וָאִמָּֽלְטָה רַק אֲנִי לְבַדִּי *I only,* [Psalm 91:8](https://ref.ly/Ps91:8) \+ oft. Once strengthening אַח, †[Nu 12:2](https://ref.ly/Num12:2) ´הֲרַק אַח־בְּמשֶׁה דִּבֶּר י:. And separated (as sts. in English) from the word actually emphasized, [Pr 13:10](https://ref.ly/Prov13:10) רִק בְּזָדוֺן יִתֵּן מַצָּה by pride there *only* cometh \[נָתַן **1 z**] contention. **b.** prefixed to *sentences,* to add a limitation on sthg. previously expressed (or implied), [Gn 19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8) *only* to these men do nothing, [24:8](https://ref.ly/Gen24:8) רִק אֶת־בְּנִי לאֹ־תָהֵ֫נָּה, [Ex 8:24](https://ref.ly/Exod8:24) I will let you go … , *only* go not far, v:[25](https://ref.ly/Exod8:25), [Nu 20:19](https://ref.ly/Num20:19) *only*—it is nothing—let me pass through on my feet (cf. [Dt 2:28](https://ref.ly/Deut2:28)); esp. in Deut. writers, as [Dt 10:15](https://ref.ly/Deut10:15), [12:15](https://ref.ly/Deut12:15), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Deut20:16), [1 K 3:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:2), [3:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:3) (cf. [2 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:4), [14:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:4), [15:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:4), [15:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:35)), [8:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:19), [11:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:13), [15:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:14), [15:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:23), [2 K 3:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:2) (cf. [14:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:3), [17:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:2)), etc., [Is 4:1](https://ref.ly/Isa4:1); emphasizing a command , [Dt 4:9](https://ref.ly/Deut4:9), [12:16](https://ref.ly/Deut12:16), [12:23](https://ref.ly/Deut12:23), [Jos 1:7](https://ref.ly/Josh1:7), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Josh1:18), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Josh6:18), [13:6](https://ref.ly/Josh13:6), [22:5](https://ref.ly/Josh22:5) (all D2). **c.** emphasizing single words, esp. adjj., *only* \= *nought but, altogether,* [Gn 6:5](https://ref.ly/Gen6:5) יֵצֶר מַחְשְׁבוֺת לִבּוֺ רִק רִע is *only* evil, i.e. *exclusively* evil, *nought but* evil, [Gn 26:29](https://ref.ly/Gen26:29) עָשִינוּ עִמְּךָ רִק טוֺב *nothing but* good, [Dt 28:33](https://ref.ly/Deut28:33) וְהָיִיתָ רִק עָשׁוּק, [Is 28:19](https://ref.ly/Isa28:19) רִק זְוָעָה it shall be *nought but* terror to, etc., [1 K 14:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:8) (cf. אַח **2 b** β); sq. an adv. [Dt 28:13](https://ref.ly/Deut28:13) וְהָיִיתָ רִק לְמַ֫עְלָה above *only;* sq. a vb., Ju [14:16](https://ref.ly/Judg14:16) thou dost *but* hate me. †**d.** after a neg., *save, except* (syn. כִּי אִם **2 a**), [1 K 8:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:9) ( \= [2 Ch 5:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:10)) אֵין בָּאָרוֺן רִק שְׁנֵי לֻהוֺת הָאֲבָנִים, [15:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:5), [22:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:16) ( \= [2 Ch 18:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:15)), [2 K 17:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:18). †**e.** with an affirmative, asseverative force, *only, altogether \= surely,* [Gn 20:11](https://ref.ly/Gen20:11) רַק אֵין יִרְאַת אלהים במקום הזה, [Dt 4:6](https://ref.ly/Deut4:6) (so EV; but Ges populus *mere* sapiens, so Di), [1 K 21:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:25), [2 Ch 28:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:10), [Psalm 32:6](https://ref.ly/Ps32:6) רִק לְשֶׁטֶף מים רבים אליו לא יִַ\+גּיעוּ. †**f.** רִק אִם (רִק prefixed for emph.) *if only, provided only,* [Dt 15:5](https://ref.ly/Deut15:5) ´רִק אִם שׁמוע תשׁמע וג:, [1 K 8:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:25) ( \= [2 Ch 6:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:16)) רִק אִם ישׁמרו בניך דרכם ´ללכת וג:, [2 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:8) ( \= [2 Ch 33:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:8)).

> \[Str7550] †רָקִיק **n.m.** a thin cake, (RV) wafer (cf. Ez [24:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:5) *cause to boil,* bring to boiling, c. acc. rei. **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 pl. רֻתְּחוּ [Jb 30:27](https://ref.ly/Job30:27) my bowels *have been made to boil* without quiet (fig. of violent emotion). **Hiph** *Impf.* 3 ms. יַרְתִּיחַ כַּסּיר מְצוּלָה [Jb 41:23](https://ref.ly/Job41:23) *he* (the crocod.) *maketh the depth boil like the pot.*.
